  text): kept as it is, shown on the card under its own label 「AI の説明（サイトから読んだ情報ではありません）」,
  never a fact and never compared at the re-read (the server hashes the fixed content, explanation
  included, so the card shows exactly what was approved). The plan then waits for the member; nothing here
  approves, claims or sends.
- **The request tool** (`electron/browser-request/tools.ts`, `browser_request` on `acb_browser`,
  `mcp__acb_browser__browser_request`): offered only when `browserRequestForTurn` gives one — the wiring
  present and the switch on — and only to an agent with `capabilities.browserOperator` (Claude Code, in
  process, `alwaysLoad`, auto-allowed), never in an unattended step. **It stops with the turn**: its signal
  follows the request's (the member's stop) and, once the turn is tracked, the turn's own (`chatTurns`: an
  administrator's stop, a job ended, a window closed) — the preview, the facts' reads and the registration
  all end. Input: `site`, `operation`,
  `target_hints`, `change_hints` (names as the user said them), `reason`. **The AI gets the plan's number and
  fixed words only** (`BROWSER_REQUEST_WORDS`): never a fact, a hash, the page's text, the operator's
  question or a run's result (decision 7); it cannot approve, run, report or confirm (no such tool). Its
  category is `web` (the emergency stop's `web` kind stops it, as `browserAiStopped`). A plan made puts
  the card's reference in the reply (`data-browser-plan`, `BrowserPlanCardRef`, stored as `browserPlans`).
- **IPC** (`plans-ipc.ts`; main window's main frame only, `isMainFrameSender`; every argument checked at
  the entry before anything is asked): `browserPlans:list` (the inbox's rows, "The inbox's rows" below: **only
  while the switch is on** — off, or signed out, it answers empty and asks the server nothing; then
  `/browser-plans-open` and the first page of `/browser-executions-waiting` together, copied by
  `readBrowserInboxPlans` — only the signed-in member's own runs (an owner's / admin's waiting list holds
  everyone's), only `permitted` / `unknown`, only the fields a row shows, an item not of its shape left out —;
  either list unreachable → offline), `browserPlans:get` (the server's view — every field the card reads checked for its
  shape and copied alone, `readBrowserPlanView`; anything else of the answer stays in main — + `ready` +
  `stoppedHere` + `runningHere` + the site's host), `browserPlans:run { companyId, planId, contentHash }` — approve + claim + the run in one
  press on this PC (decision 5), nothing else ever reaches the runner (no fact, target or AI word), one run
  per plan at a time —, `browserPlans:cancel`, `browserPlans:confirm { executionId, done | not_done }`.
- **Whether ［任せる］ can be pressed** (`browserCardReadiness`, decision 4): the switch on, the plan a
  draft, the site listed by the member's current policy, the AI not stopped, the site's window open here,
  and the page (read through the window's page-less read session with the checker's declared reads)
  showing the plan's own account; else a plain reason on the card (no window → 「開いてログイン」). Only a
  hint for the button: the runner checks everything again after the press. **Showing a draft card
  therefore reads the site** — the checker's declared reads each time a draft card is shown (the 15 s
  re-read is for unsettled executions only, which make no site read) — only while the switch is on and the AI not stopped (owner OK,
  2026-11-22).
- **The card** (`client/src/features/browser-plans/`: `components/BrowserPlanCard.tsx` opened by
  `{ companyId, planId }` from anywhere — the chat's reply and the inbox's row —, `planStore.ts` +
  `hooks/useBrowserPlan.ts` (read whenever a card shows it again — cards shown together share one read —,
  no effect of its own; an unsettled execution — claimed, permitted, unknown — is read again every 15 s,
  so the server's time-outs and the confirm's opening show without a restart), `model.ts` the words,
  `api.ts` small ports. Decision (safe side, the IPC is main window only): in a consultation's own window
  the card shows only 「このカードは、アプリのメインの窓の相談で開いてください」. The copy is kept per
  signed-in member, through the server being out of reach only — a refusal or not-found drops it). Read from the server's fixed view only: what / where / to whom / when under 「サイトから読み取った
  情報」 — the account, the reminder and the step, the templates on the step and the one to add (name and
  body), 「届く時」 as the page shows it (never parsed) with 「この段の時刻をこれから迎える人に、その時刻に
  届きます。すでに過ぎた人には届きません。」 for `delivers: scheduled`, 「これから届く人数」 N人 or
  **「読み取れませんでした」 in amber, never 0** (decision 1), the 回 using it, the read time —, the
  declaration's shared note as a warning callout, then 「AI の説明（サイトから読んだ情報ではありません）」.
  Amber while awaiting approval. After a run: 「計画どおりの状態になっています。」 / 「送る前に止めました。この
  操作の送信は行っていません。」 / 「…送りませんでした。」 (with the reason while the press is fresh) /
  **「届いたか分かりません。」 with what to check in the site** (the reminder, the step, the template) and
  ［済んでいた］［されていなかった］ only from `confirmableAt` (before: 「HH:MM から…」); this PC's record
  as a hint only (decision 10). **No resend button anywhere** (decision 6): 「もう一度は送りません。もう一度
  やるときは、相談で頼み直してください。」. Never: 「安全です」, a hash, a token, the secret.
- **Remaining limits** (said): the readiness read is a hint (a logout between it and the press stops the
  run before the permit with `account-unknown`); `browser_request` is Claude Code only (Codex has no
  operator).
- **The inbox's rows** (`notifications.md` "The inbox"; `src/features/inbox/model.ts` `browserRows`,
  `hooks/useInboxBrowserPlans.ts`): AppShell hands the inbox slot the list port and the card
  (`browserPlans`) whenever a company is open (composed by AppShell, so no feature-to-feature import);
  **main decides** — switched off on this PC, `browserPlans:list` answers empty and asks the server
  nothing, so there is no row (6c: the switch is per PC, which the renderer cannot know by itself). A draft → あなた待ち amber 「承認を待っています」
  ［承認する…］ (with the approvals); an `unknown` run the server says can be confirmed now (its `canConfirm`,
  never this PC's clock) → あなた待ち amber 「届いたか分かりません」 ［確かめる…］ (right after the stops); an approved
  plan not started, a `permitted` run and an `unknown` one not yet confirmable → shown under 新着, never counted (作業中 before
  2026-11-26). Every row only opens this
  card beside the inbox's list (the type guard in `model.test.ts` refuses an action kind that approves, sends, runs
  or confirms). Settled = in neither list = gone (nothing stored; no 新着, the server keeps no notice for
  plans). **Read** when the inbox mounts, when the panel opens, when a card in the inbox closes, **when a plan's
  state moves on any card** (`planStore` `subscribePlanStates`: ［任せる］ in the consultation's card, a cancel, a
  confirm, a settled result — not a card's first look or the same state read again), a second after the next
  `confirmableAt`, and **every 60 s while any plan or run is listed and 3 minutes after the last answer that listed one** (`PLANS_REREAD_MS`, `PLANS_GRACE_MS`: a run claimed on another PC is in neither list until its permit, which the server waits for at most 2 minutes; then no timer);
  offline keeps the rows shown, another failure clears them. Limits (said): a plan made in a consultation shows
  at the next of those reads (no push for plans); **only the waiting list's first page is read** (100, oldest
  claim first: an admin with more open runs of others may not see their own there; the open list's `more` is
  not told).

Tests: `client/tests/browser-plans-app.test.ts` (the switch off: no preview, read or server call — the inbox's list empty with no request —; the inbox's list: the member's own runs only, the row's fields only, a bad list refused, either list offline; a plan
registered with this build's combination and the checker's facts, the AI's words only as the explanation;
a required fact unread, the context moving, the emergency stop or the turn's stop during the reads or
right after the preview, no combination, the operator not finding it → nothing registered; the plan view
copied to the card only in its shape; the tool's fixed words and no operator question; the secret only in its header and
the report without a session; ids outside the server's shapes never sent; 4xx / 5xx / thrown; the IPC from
another window or with bad arguments asks nothing, ［任せる］ hands exactly company, plan and hash, one run
per plan; readiness by switch, state, site, stop, window and account; the context's generation; the
combination's checks; as it ships nothing reaches a site, the operator or the server and the turn gets no
tool; switched on, the tool hands the request the turn's company, consultation and signal),
`agent-stop.test.ts` (an administrator's stop of the turn reaches the request's signal), `src/features/browser-plans/model.test.ts` (an unread count never 0; awaiting only for a draft; a
result nobody knows says what to check, no resend, confirm only after its time; plain reasons),
`client/tests/ui/browser-plan-card.spec.ts` (preview `browser-plan-card-ui.html`: the facts, the
timing sentence, 「読み取れませんでした」, the shared warning; ［任せる］ sends only the plan and the shown
hash; disabled with the reason and 「開いてログイン」; ［やめる］; unknown before and after its time, the
confirm opening by itself on a fake clock; the plain results; no jargon), `src/features/browser-plans/planStore.test.ts`
(a card shown again reads again, a failure recovers, two cards share one read; a copy survives only the server being out of reach; a state moving is told, a first look or the same state is not), `browser-runner.test.ts` (the one way in). Each guard mutation-checked; second
layer kept on purpose: the registration's context check before each read (the check after each answer
catches the same change).

## This PC's opt-in (6c: on for the owner's PC only; since D2 the transition's exception)

Owner decision 2026-11-22: the AI's browser operation is turned on **only on the owner's PC** for the
real-machine check (design memo §33 1-6) and the first real run; everyone else stays off, also members
running the very same build. Design memo §33.3 prescribes the server's PC registration for 6c; for one
PC whose person is the company's owner, this PR uses the smallest mechanism that keeps the build off for
everyone else, and leaves that registration as the condition before anyone else is turned on ("Not yet").

**Since D2 ("This PC's registration") this file is the transition's exception** (design memo
「ブラウザ操作を担当者の PC で使う」 v2 §10): the server now asks every gated step for a registered PC, and lets a
request **without** the PC's key header through for an owner only (`BROWSER_DEVICE_OWNER_TRANSITION`) — exactly
what this file does today, so the owner's PC keeps working on it until the app that sends the header (D4) is
everywhere. A member or an admin with the file is refused by the server (`BROWSER_DEVICE_NOT_REGISTERED`), so the
weakness below no longer reaches them. D5 removes the file and the exception together, after D4 has reached every PC
and the owner's PC is registered.

- **The build constant stays `false`** (`BROWSER_AI_BUILD_SWITCH`): no build is on for everyone. main
  composes `createThisPcSwitch({ file: <userData>/browser-ai-this-pc.json, server, userId })`
  (`features/browser/ai-switch.ts`) and, since D4, folds it into `createRegisteredPcSwitch` (on when the opt-in **or**
  this PC's enabled registration says so: "This PC's registration", the app's half) — the switch every wiring gets.
- **The opt-in** is a file the person writes **by hand** in the app's userData folder (never a company
  folder), exactly `{ "browserAi": "this-pc", "server": "<origin>", "userId": "<id>", "until": "<ISO
  time with zone>" }` (`browserAiOptInOn`, `client/shared/browser-ai.ts`, pure): on only while `server`
  is the app's server (origin compared), `userId` the signed-in member, and `until` in the future and at
  most 14 days ahead (`BROWSER_AI_OPT_IN_MAX_DAYS`: a forgotten opt-in ends by itself). Any other key,
  value or shape, more than 4 KB, a link (`O_NOFOLLOW`), a folder, a pipe (`O_NONBLOCK`, so main's thread
  never waits) or a file that cannot be read = off. No setting, IPC or screen writes it: nothing in the
  app can turn itself on.
- **Read on every ask** (no cache): removing the file, `until` passing, signing in as another member or
  to another server turns everything off at once — the request tool, the inbox's list, ［任せる］, the
  card's readiness — and, through the context (`context.ts` asks the switch on every check), **a run or a
  registration in progress**: the next check stops it (before the permit: the plan given back; at the
  last check before the request leaves: `failed`, never sent).
- **Everything else still decides** (unchanged): the company's setting 「使う」 with the site listed in the
  admin console and the member's override (`browserOperationsAllowed`, app and server), the AI stop,
  the member's **type grant** (`PUT /browser-type-grants/:userId`, owner / admin, per declaration hash —
  no admin page yet: the 6c notes give the call), the approved combination (`approved.json` `approved`
  with its `minAppVersion`; `lstep@0.6.1` approved in this PR, `lstep@0.6.2` since "Login helpers", `lstep@0.6.3` since "Asset hosts"), the card's approval
  and the re-read before the permit. Turning off: delete the file, or the company's setting 「使わない」 /
  the site removed (the context moves, the server's permit refuses), or the emergency stop.
- **Weakness (said)**: **the per-PC file is not access control — the server's type grant is.** The file
  is the person's own; an AI turn outside the sandbox (`record-only`, the API key's agent) could write it
  as it could read §33.3's device file. It only narrows where it is on; the grant and the person's
  approval on the card are the guards. Before D2, **granting a second member turned them on wherever
  they wrote the file**; since D2 the server refuses a member's or an admin's step without a registered PC,
  so the file only ever reaches the owner (the transition's exception above).
- **The operator's init check, accepted as built in** (owner decision 2026-11-22, "Checked on every
  start"): built-in plugins are matched by **pattern** (`path: 'builtin'`, `source: '<name>@builtin'`), not
  pinned by name — a later CLI's new built-in plugin passes (consistent with "what Anthropic ships is
  trusted"); the agents are an **exact list** — a member's agent with one of those names cannot be told
  apart, but the operator's flags load no member agents and with `--tools ''` none can be started. `BROWSER_NOT_YET_NOTE` in the
  admin page stays (true for every PC but the owner's).

Tests: `client/tests/browser-ai-opt-in.test.ts` (an opt-in only for its server, member and until its end,
14 days at most, exactly its shape; the file read on every ask — removed = off at once, a link, a folder,
a pipe, an unworkable path = off; the same build without the opt-in — the company's setting on, the site
listed, an opt-in for another member — reaches no site, operator or server and offers no tool; with it on,
the tool exists and the inbox asks the server, and the opt-in removed while the approval is on its way
stops the run before the claim), `browser-plans-app.test.ts` (a capture reads false once the switch is
off), `browser-execute.test.ts` (the opt-in removed between the permit and the send, with the switch and
context composed as main does: the last check refuses, failed, nothing reaches the site). Each guard mutation-checked; second layer kept on purpose: the regular-file check after
`O_NOFOLLOW` / `O_NONBLOCK` (a folder or a pipe already fails to read).

## This PC's registration (D2: the server's half; D4: the app's half)

Owner-approved design memo 「ブラウザ操作を担当者の PC で使う」 v2 (2026-11-24) §3–§8, §10, §14-1 (「必要最低限に絞る」: everything
in §14-3 「あとから足せる」 stays out — a confirmation number, expiries, extra checks on one's own PC, per-company keys, the
personal MCP's visibility, a lost-PC guide, binding an inspection's result to the PC). Owner answers to §12: the
transition's exception is for an owner only; an inspection runs only while the member has that consultation open (the
app's, L1). The server decides **from which PC** a plan's steps come; the person's session, the grant and the card stay
the guards they were.

- **Pieces.** `shared/core/browser-device.ts` (pure: `BROWSER_DEVICE_HEADER` `X-ACB-Browser-Device`,
  `parseBrowserDeviceKey` — exactly `v1.` + 32 bytes in unpadded base64url, the last character's unused bits zero so a key
  has one spelling —, the states `pending` / `enabled` / `revoked` and their moves, the revoke reasons `admin` / `self` /
  `left`, `normalizeBrowserDeviceLabel` (NFC, trimmed, 1–60, no control or direction character), the refusal words
  `BROWSER_DEVICE_WORDS` (plain Japanese, 「PC」 never 「デバイス」), `BROWSER_DEVICE_OWNER_TRANSITION` and the gate's one rule
  `browserDeviceDecision`); `shared/types/browser-devices.ts`; server `routes/browser-devices.ts` →
  `services/browser-devices.ts` → the handle's `browserDevices` (`ports/browser-devices.ts`,
  `adapters/sqlite/browser-devices.ts`, tables in `db/schema/browser-devices.ts`). The plan services get the gate
  injected (`BrowserDeviceGate`: `check`, `bind`; composed in `composition.ts`), never a row or a hash.
- **Tables** (new only; no column of a plan table changes): `browser_devices` (company, `bd_` + 32 hex id, member,
  **`key_hash` = sha256 of the header value — the key itself is never stored, logged or answered**, the PC's name,
  state, revoke reason, requested / enabled (by) / revoked (by) / last used; one registration not stopped per company ×
  member × key — partial unique index —; trigger: the fixed columns never change, only pending → enabled → revoked,
  revoked final; CHECKs tie who / when to the state; no DELETE while its company exists), `browser_device_history` (append-only: which registration, whose,
  who acted, `request` / `enable` / `revoke`, the reason, when), `browser_plan_devices` (company × path `type` | `line` ×
  plan → the registration that made it; written once with the plan, in its transaction; never changed or removed while
  its company exists; no row = made under the owner's exception or before D2).
- **Routes** (`/api/companies/:companyId`, a person's session): `POST /browser-devices { label }` + the header (this
  member registers this PC: `pending`, 201; asked again for a registration not stopped → that one, 200; at most
  `BROWSER_DEVICE_LIMITS.livePerMember` (10) not stopped → `BROWSER_DEVICE_LIMIT`), `GET /browser-devices-me` + the
  header (this member's registration of this PC — the one not stopped, else the newest stopped one — or `{ state: 'none' }`;
  `no-store`), `DELETE /browser-devices-me` + the header (取り下げる / この PC での利用をやめる: `revoked`, `self`), `GET
  /browser-devices` (owner/admin: every registration, newest first, 500 with `more`, the person's name, the history's
  newest 200; never a key or its hash), `POST /browser-devices/:deviceId/enable` (owner/admin, from `pending` only, else
  409 `BROWSER_DEVICE_STATE_CHANGED`; **their own PC too, one press, no further check** — owner decision 11/24),
  `POST /browser-devices/:deviceId/revoke` (owner/admin; a stopped one answered as it is). A missing or malformed header is
  400 `BROWSER_DEVICE_BAD_KEY`; another company's registration is the same 404 `BROWSER_DEVICE_NOT_FOUND` as none (the
  handle). `GET /browser-lines-mine` (any member, `routes/browser-lines.ts`): the approved lines they may use now (their
  level on the site allows the line's effect: "Who may use a site") — ref, label, `writes` — for the app's 「この PC」 and
  the request tool (D4, L1).
- **The gate** (`devices.check`, the **first** check of the old path's `gate` and the new path's `gate` — so register /
  approve / claim / permit and create / approve / claim / every permit; the route passes the header): no header → only
  an **owner** passes, with no registration (the transition's exception: "This PC's opt-in"); a header that is not one
  key → `BROWSER_DEVICE_NOT_REGISTERED` (never the exception); this member's registration of that key in this company:
  none → `BROWSER_DEVICE_NOT_REGISTERED`, `pending` → `BROWSER_DEVICE_PENDING`, `revoked` → `BROWSER_DEVICE_REVOKED`
  (each 403), `enabled` → passes; then for a step of an existing plan **its registration must be this one** (none for
  none) → else 409 `BROWSER_DEVICE_ELSEWHERE` (another PC of the member, or this PC registered again: a new
  registration, so an old plan never moves again). A step that passes the PC check notes `last_used_at` at most once a day (before the rest of the gate: it says the PC asked, not that the step went through). The check at a plan's creation runs before the plan's insert transaction; a PC stopped in between leaves a plan bound to a stopped registration, which every later step refuses. A
  refusal at a permit ends the execution as any gate refusal (old path `void` reason `device`; new path `void` /
  `partial` with the code). **Not asked** (clean-up never stops): cancel (so another PC can only 「やめる」), reports,
  confirms, an inspection's result (binding it to the PC is §14-3), the lists, the shape notice.
- **Stopping**: an owner / admin's 止める (`admin`), the member's own (`self`), and **leaving the company** — every
  registration of the person stopped (`left`) in `companies.removeMember`'s transaction (`onMemberLeft` →
  `browserDevices.memberLeft`); invited back, nothing returns (a new registration starts `pending`). A stop applies to the
  next gated step at once (the server is the authority); a permit already given (5 s) may still be used, and a write
  already sent is settled by its report or a person.
- **Weaknesses (said, §3-4)**: the key is not bound to hardware — an AI outside the sandbox that the person lets read
  the keychain item could read it; the guards remain the grant and the person's approval on the card. A stolen owner /
  admin session can register and enable its own PC (no confirmation number: §14-3). The member's own browser MCPs are
  outside this (their visibility is §14-3). The request log prints `deviceId` (an opaque id), never the header.

- **The app's half (D4)** (design memo v2 §3-2, §7-1, §8-2): `client/electron/features/browser/device.ts`
  (`createBrowserDeviceKey`: 32 random bytes as `v1.` + base64url, made the first time the member registers, **kept only
  sealed by the OS keychain** — `SecretBox`, safeStorage handed in by main.ts as for the API key — in
  `<userData>/browser-device-key-<sha256(origin)[0..16]>` — **one key per server origin**, the sealed text naming its
  origin (independent review: the key one server trusts is never sent to another server the app is pointed at; a file
  sealed for another origin is no key) — (0600, a fresh `O_EXCL` temporary file synced and renamed, a link never
  followed), read once and then held in main's memory; no keychain (`available()` false, Linux without one) → nothing
  made, the screen 「この PC では使えません（安全な保存ができません）。」 and registering refused in that sentence; a sealed key that
  cannot be opened (another computer, a reset or denied keychain) = none, and registering again keeps that file aside
  (`.unreadable-<time>`, never overwritten) and makes a new key = a new registration. `createBrowserDevice`: `GET
  /browser-devices-me`, `POST /browser-devices { label }`, `DELETE /browser-devices-me` with the header, `GET
  /browser-lines-mine` without it; the remembered state per server × member × company — kept only for the scope it was
  asked for and not across a sign-out (an answer arriving later is dropped), forgotten on a refusal (left the company), kept
  on no answer —, asked again when the settings open, after each press and when the company's policy is answered (at
  most once a minute per company, only once this PC has a key); cleared on sign-out).
  **The header goes only** on those PC routes and, through `deviceKey` handed to the plan clients, on the steps the
  gate asks: old path register / approve / claim / permit (`plans-api.ts`), new path a plan's creation (inspection or
  writes) / approve / claim / every permit (`lines-api.ts`) — never on get, the lists, cancel, confirm, a result, a
  notice or a report, and **never on any other request main makes** (no global fetch wrapper: the server's
  `routes/broker-relay.ts` forwards request headers to outside services). A PC without a key sends none, so the owner's
  hand-placed opt-in keeps working through the server's exception; **once this PC has a key it always sends it** (a
  pending or stopped registration is then refused by the server even for the owner — the stricter reading of §10).
  The switch (`createRegisteredPcSwitch`): the opt-in file (until D5) **or** "this PC is `enabled` for the signed-in member
  on this server" in some company (synchronous, from the remembered state; another company's steps are still the server's
  to refuse). IPC `browserDevice:get` / `register` / `withdraw` (`device-ipc.ts`, main window's main frame only) answer a
  `BrowserPcView` (`client/shared/browser-device.ts`): the state (`none` / `pending` / `enabled` / `revoked` /
  `unavailable` / `unknown`), the PC's name, a suggested name (this computer's), the combinations' labels and whether they
  write — **never the key, its hash or the registration's id**. Settings › 「ブラウザ操作」 › 「この PC」
  (`src/features/settings/components/BrowserPcSection.tsx`, `hooks/useBrowserPc.ts`; words `BROWSER_PC_WORDS`): each
  state in plain Japanese with what to do next (確認待ち: 「管理者に「登録しました」と伝えて、使えるようにしてもらってください。」),
  the usable combinations (「使える作業はまだありません。管理者に使えるようにしてもらってください。」 when none), 「この PC を登録する」
  with the PC's name (shown whatever the combinations: the owner's old-path grant is not one of them), 「取り下げる」 /
  「この PC での利用をやめる」 behind a danger confirm, 「登録し直す」 once stopped. The section shows wherever the 「ブラウザ操作」
  item does (the company's setting on with a site). No confirmation number, expiry or extra check (§14-3).

Tests: `server/tests/unit/browser-device-core.test.ts` (the key's one spelling, the name, the moves, the gate's rule as
a table: every state × role × plan binding, the exception for an owner only and none once it ends),
`server/tests/unit/browser-devices.test.ts` (the real device and opened-lines services: registering twice, after a stop,
the name and the limit; enable / revoke owner / admin only, an admin's own PC, from pending only, revoked final and the
trigger, another company's id the same 404; the list without any key or hash; no key material in any table; the new
path's gate — none, pending, enabled, stopped, another member's or company's registration of the same PC, a malformed
key, the owner's exception and nobody else's, a demoted owner, the PC first —; last used once a day; the plan bound to
its PC — another PC, the same PC registered again, the owner's plan without a PC, a permit from another PC voiding the
execution, cancel from another PC —; leaving; `browser-lines-mine`), `server/tests/integration/browser-plans.test.ts`
(over HTTP with the real composition: members register and are enabled, an admin without a PC refused and the owner's
exception, a plan bound to its PC, a revoked PC voiding the claimed execution (`device`), the second PC cancelling,
`browser-devices-me` uncached and own only, the real member removal stopping the PC), the old and new paths' own tests
(with a stand-in PC check, `tests/helpers/browser-devices.ts`), permission matrix rows + `CROSS`,
`company-scoped-sql.test.ts`, `jobs-schema.test.ts`. Each guard mutation-checked (32 mutations; one survivor kept on purpose: enable's own
`pending` check, behind the compare-and-set and the trigger, each of which refuses the same). The app's half: `client/tests/browser-device.test.ts` (the key's
one spelling every time; made once, sealed — no plain key on disk, 0600 —, read back after a restart, another keychain's
or a linked file = none; one key per server (another server gets none, a file sealed for another origin is none); a key
the keychain will not open kept aside, never overwritten; an answer arriving after a sign-out or another sign-in never
remembered; a refusal forgets 使える, no answer keeps it; only `device.ts` and the two plan clients name the header and
only main.ts and the plan wirings hand the key on (a source scan); no keychain: nothing made or sent, the plain sentence; registering sends the key in the header
and the name alone in the body, the answers never hold the key or the id, the usable list asked without it; a refusal's
sentence, no answer `unknown`; the refresh once a minute and only with a key; the old and new plan clients carry it on
exactly the gated steps; no key → no header and the opt-in alone turns the switch on), `client/tests/ipc-browser-device.test.ts`
(the real main.ts: every request main made carries the key only on the PC routes, never in an answer, a log, an address,
a body or on disk in the clear; another window refused; no keychain refused), `client/tests/ui/browser-settings.spec.ts`
(「この PC」 on `download-folders-ui.html?tab=browser&browser=1&pc=…`: the states, the combinations, register with the name,
stop behind the confirm, none / no keychain / no server, a refusal's sentence). Mutation-checked (the gated header on
each client, the main-window check; survivor kept on purpose: `register`'s own keychain check, behind `ensure()`
refusing the same).

## The admin pages: PCs and people (PR A)

Design memo v2 §7-2, §14 (minimum only: no carry-over helper, no extra check). Two pages under 「詳しい設定 › AI の安全 ›
ブラウザ操作」, linked from that page only (two links at its top); owner/admin only — a member or a department manager gets
「このページはオーナーと管理者だけが使えます」 and nothing is asked (the server also refuses: 403). Words:
`admin/src/lib/agent-browser-people.ts` (「PC」, 「使えるようにする」, 「作業」; never 「開ける」, no hash or version on screen).
Types from `shared/types/browser-devices.ts` / `browser-lines.ts` via `admin/src/lib/api.ts` (`browserPcsApi`,
`browserPeopleApi`), pinned by `admin-contract.test.ts`.

- **「ブラウザ操作に使う PC」** (`…/browser/pcs`): a table of every registration — person, PC name, 申し込んだ日, state
  (確認待ち `awaiting` / 使える `success` / 止めた `paused` with the reason), 最後に使った日 — and the history (who, when,
  which PC, what). ［使えるようにする］ only on 確認待ち (a plain confirm naming the person and the PC, owner/admin's own PC
  too); ［止める］ on 確認待ち and 使える, always behind a destructive confirm that says what stops (`PC_STOP_EFFECTS`: new
  requests refused at once, the one being sent may go, a run stops before its next write, registering again to use it).
  One call per press, to that PC's id.
- **「ブラウザ操作を使える人」** (`…/browser/people`): since step ① rows = members, columns = the registered sites,
  cell = 使わない / 見るだけ / 変えてよい ("Who may use a site"; before, one column per site × line, which showed one site's
  jobs to every company). Notes: 「使えるようにした PC はまだありません」 (link to the PCs page) for a person with no
  `enabled` PC; the company's setting 「使わない」 above the table (naming the member override as the exception).
- Not done (memo §7-2, left for later): 「変えています」 on 「詳しい設定」 for PCs / grants, a per-person 「使わない」 note.

Tests: `admin/tests/agent-browser-people.test.tsx` (the links; a member / manager sees neither page and nothing is asked;
each state and what may be pressed; enable and stop ask first and send one call to that PC; キャンセル sends nothing; 「使える人」:
"Who may use a site"), `server/tests/integration/admin-contract.test.ts`. Guards mutation-checked (the stop's confirm).
Administrator guide: 13節 「使う PC と使える人」 (scene `browser-pcs`).

## Who may use a site (step ① of the redesign: domain × 見るだけ／変えてよい)

Owner-approved design memo 「ブラウザ操作：記録から自動化へ／サイトの言葉を本体から外す」 (2026-11-25) §3 I5 / I10, §10, §11,
§12 row ①, §13 D4 / D7. The per-line grant made every company's 「使える人」 list one site's jobs (a design failure: a site's
words must never appear in a core screen, an AI tool's description or a refusal shared by all companies). The core keeps
generic units only: **the host the company registered × 使わない / 見るだけ / 変えてよい**.

- **Rule** (`shared/core/browser-operation.ts`): `BrowserPersonLevel` `none` | `read` | `write`,
  `BROWSER_PERSON_DEFAULT_LEVEL` = `read`, `browserPersonAllows(person, siteLevel, effect)` (both must allow: `write`
  lets the person do what the company's level allows, `read` only reads, `none` / anything unknown nothing),
  `effectiveBrowserPersonLevel` (what the admin page shows as applying).
- **Table** `browser_site_grants` (additive, in `db/schema/browser-lines.ts`, behind the browser lines' handle as
  `BrowserSiteGrantRepository`): company × user × host (the company's very spelling) × level (CHECK), `granted_by`,
  `updated_at`. **No row = 見るだけ** (owner decision D4: everyone starts at 見るだけ once the site is registered; an admin
  may set 使わない); 変えてよい only by a row, one person at a time. A row counts only when written while the person is a
  member this time (`updated_at` ≥ the membership's `created_at`); leaving removes the rows (`memberLeft`); someone not a
  member gets nothing. A row for a host no longer registered counts nowhere (and is not shown). **A host removed, or
  lowered below 入力・保存まで, clears its 変えてよい rows in the setting's save transaction** (`services/agent-browser.ts`
  `hostsNoLongerWritable` → `writeNoLongerAllowed`): raising the site or registering it again never brings a 変えてよい
  back unseen; 使わない / 見るだけ rows stay. Owners and admins follow the same table (no implicit 変えてよい).
- **Service** `services/browser-site-grants.ts` (`createBrowserSiteGrants`, built in `composition.ts` and injected into
  the line service as `siteGrants`): `allows(member, host, effect)` = the company's mode with the member's override on, the
  host registered, `browserPersonAllows(own level, site level, effect)`. `view` / `set` (owner/admin, else 403
  `manageForbidden`): `GET /browser-site-grants` → `BrowserSiteGrantsView` (`shared/types/browser-site-grants.ts`: the
  registered sites with their level, `defaultLevel`, the rows in force); `PUT /browser-site-grants/:userId { host, level }`
  — the user must be a member (404), exactly these two keys and a known level (400 `BROWSER_BAD_SITE_GRANT`), the host
  exactly a registered one (400 `BROWSER_SITE_NOT_REGISTERED`), `write` only on a site whose level allows writing (409
  `BROWSER_SITE_LEVEL_ABOVE_SITE`; a row set before the company lowered the level stays but is capped). Answers the whole
  view.
- **The line gate** (create, approve, claim, every permit; "Plans of opened lines on the server") asks `allows` for the
  line's site host and effect (an inspection `read`: 見るだけ is enough — D7 keeps `landing_inspect` working; writes
  `write`) where it asked the per-line grant; the refusal code stays `BROWSER_TYPE_NOT_GRANTED` (released apps map it),
  its sentence names no job. The shape notice and `GET /browser-lines-mine` ask the same. **The old path asks it too**
  (`services/browser-plans.ts` `gate`, register / approve / claim / every permit, after its per-type grant: both are
  needed, the operation's effect against the person's level) — so 使わない / 見るだけ on 「使える人」 stops the old path's
  plans and permits as well (a refusal at a permit voids the execution, reason `not-granted`). Its per-type grants
  (`browser_type_grants`, `lstep@0.6.x`) stay until step ⑦.
- **The per-line grants are kept readable, never consulted**: `browser_line_grants` stays (no column or row changes;
  released builds and self-hosters' rows), a line grant alone allows nothing; `GET /browser-line-grants` answers its old
  shape **empty** (`{ lines: [], waiting: [], grants: [] }`, owner/admin; an older admin console then shows no job), `PUT
  /browser-line-grants/:userId` answers 410 `BROWSER_LINE_GRANTS_REPLACED` to owners / admins (403 to others, as before).
  Only the admin console read them (the desktop app reads `/browser-lines-mine`).
- **Admin 「ブラウザ操作を使える人」** (`…/browser/people`, words `admin/src/lib/agent-browser-people.ts`): rows = members,
  columns = registered hosts with the company's level, cell = a select 使わない / 見るだけ / 変えてよい (見るだけ when nobody
  chose; 変えてよい disabled on a 見るだけ site; 「いまは会社の設定で「見るだけ」です」 under a capped row). A choice is one
  `PUT` at once. No job, line, hash or version on screen.

Tests: `server/tests/unit/browser-site-grants.test.ts` (another company's row ignored, an unregistered host never allowed
nor settable, 見るだけ cannot write, 使わない cannot read, the ceiling, the company off, a member who left / a row from before
they came back, owners / admins only and no implicit 変えてよい, the view, the body), `browser-lines.test.ts` (the gate's
order with the person's level, the old per-line grant alone allowing nothing and its routes naming no line, 見るだけ cannot
make a write plan, `browser-lines-mine` listing a writing line only to 変えてよい, leaving), `browser-devices.test.ts` (`browser-lines-mine` per level), permission matrix rows + `CROSS`,
`admin-contract.test.ts`, `company-scoped-sql.test.ts`, `jobs-schema.test.ts`, `browser-plans.test.ts` (the old path: the type
grant alone not enough, 見るだけ / 使わない / the default refused at register, a lowered level voiding a claimed execution),
`integration/agent-browser.test.ts` (a site lowered or removed clears its 変えてよい, raised keeps it),
`admin/tests/agent-browser-people.test.tsx`
(fictional sites: the cells, one `PUT` per choice, the cap, no site registered, no job words). Each guard mutation-checked.

## The tools' words (step ①: no site word in what every company ships)

- The AI's browser tools (`browser_inspect`, `browser_write`, `browser_request`) describe themselves in shared words;
  their schemas say "as listed in the description". **What can be asked** — site, line / operation, value or hint names
  with their headings — is a catalog built per turn from the site data the build carries
  (`client/shared/browser-tool-catalog.ts`: `browserLineCatalog` over the bundled lines whose opened row is approved on a
  site whose approved declaration the build carries; `browserOperationCatalog` over the approved declarations'
  operations), **only for sites the turn's company registered** (its current valid policy: `siteLevel` / `siteNow`), and
  appended to the description (`description` on each tool object, read by `agents/claude.ts`). A company that registered
  none of them, or a build without a site's data, hears no site word (`BROWSER_CATALOG_NONE`).
- Refusal and tool words in `client/shared` (`BROWSER_INSPECT_WORDS`, `BROWSER_REQUEST_WORDS`), the server's browser
  refusals and the admin console's texts name no site.
- **Test** `client/tests/browser-core-words.test.ts` (extends §17-2 to texts): the string literals, template text and JSX
  text of `client/shared`, `client/src`, `shared/core`, `shared/types`, `client/electron/browser-request`,
  `client/electron/features/browser`, `admin/src` and the server's browser services and routes hold none of a **fictional site sample**'s words nor any name the
  carried site data gives (approved declarations, opened maps and lines; the everyday words 「タグ」「アクション」 alone are
  excepted, a site's phrase holding them is not); the tools' descriptions and schemas with an empty catalog hold none,
  and the sample's words reach a tool only through the catalog. `EXCLUDED`, each with its reason: the site data kept
  until step ⑦ (`client/connector-maps/lstep`, `client/browser-lines/lstep.ts`, `shared/browser-connectors/opened.json`);
  `OLD_PATH`, removed with the old path in step ⑦: `shared/core/browser-plans.ts` (its one checker's facts), the old plan
  card `src/features/browser-plans/components/BrowserPlanCard.tsx` and its `model.ts` (`client/connectors/lstep/` is
  outside the scanned folders).
- Tests of the filter: `client/tests/browser-inspect-app.test.ts` and `browser-plans-app.test.ts` (another company of
  the same member that registered nothing: no line, operation, site or host in the description).

## Recording (「記録する」, step ③ of the redesign: a person records the work once; the FDE builds a company Skill from it)

Owner decisions 2026-11-25 (internal design memo 「ブラウザ操作：記録から自動化へ」 §3 I7–I9, §4, §10 「記録」, §11 `browser_recordings`,
§12 row ③, D2, D3): a member who knows the work presses 「記録する」 on a site in Settings › 「ブラウザ操作」, does the work once in
the site's window, stops, looks through the lines, deletes what they do not want and presses ［送る］. The company's owners /
admins (the FDE enters a company as an admin) read it in the admin console and build a company Skill from it. **A recording
reaches an AI only as its sender's own** (since "A Skill's draft from a recording" PR 2, owner decision D1): the sender's
consultation AI reads the sender's own recordings through the drafts' routes; no record token, and no AI tool calls the
routes below, where an owner / admin reads everyone's.

- **What is kept** (`shared/core/browser-recording.ts`, pure, the one copy the app writes with and the server checks with;
  tightened after the independent review of this PR): **a word is kept only as a plain lowercase ASCII word** joined by
  `-` / `_` (a leading `_` too), ≤ 24 — anything with a digit, a capital, a dot, `@`, `%`, a character not ASCII or
  longer becomes `{x}` whole (all digits `{n}`), never digit by digit. That rule applies to every path segment and query
  key (`browserAddressPattern`: the query's keys only, no fragment, user or port; another host → none) and to every part
  of a key — a JSON key, a form name's `name[part][part]` (`browserKeyPattern`) — so `/users/yamada.taro`,
  `/invite/Kx7Pq2Rt9mZ`, a letters-only token with capitals, `user[Tanaka]` and camelCase keys are masked; **an object
  with more than 5 keys whose values share one shape** (customers by name) keeps them as `{k}` (`browserJsonShape`).
  The labels of buttons, choices and fields (`browserLabel`: NFKC, whitespace collapsed, e-mail-like text → `{mail}`, an
  address → `{url}`, **every digit run** → `{n}`, ≤ 60); each value as a **symbol of this recording** (`値N`; `empty`,
  `secret` for a password field, `file` for an upload); requests: method, `_method` override (PUT / PATCH / DELETE),
  address pattern, **whether its query was sent exactly as kept** (`bareQuery`, format 2: names only, plain words, once, in
  order — `?return` true, `?preview=false` / `?a&a` / an encoding false; `browserQueryKeptExactly`; the only query a Skill's
  draft may name, "A Skill's draft from a recording"), the body's field names and symbols (form, JSON key paths, multipart; an unreadable body → `null`),
  the answer's status and JSON key shape; the other hosts the window's pages asked for (`loaded` = one answered).
  **Inferred links are marked `推定`**: a request's `after` (the person's action within 3 s before it) and a value's `from`
  (the same value first seen in that step's answer, at that key; values of 2+ characters only). A GET of the same pattern is
  one line with its `count`; a page's own load and its parts (scripts, styles, images, fonts) are not lines. A lowercase
  name (`/search/tanaka`) still passes the word rule: **the person hides it** before sending (below).
- **Never kept**: a password field's content (the page script never even reads it), Cookie, Authorization or any header,
  CSRF / token / session / password / key fields — dropped whole, judged on the raw name **before** masking
  (`isSensitiveFieldName`, a wide net: `pass`, `pwd`, `pw`, `secret`, `token`, `csrf`, `xsrf`, `session`, `auth`, `api key`,
  `otp`, `cookie`, `credential`, `signature`, `nonce`, `captcha`), the same in an answer's keys —, values, a link's text (a link
  is its address pattern only), page text and HTML, an answer's values, screenshots or video (D3: not in the first version).
  The symbols are **not an anonymisation** (said, Codex point 6): they show a flow (「値3 を一覧から読み、値3 を送った」); a label
  can still name what the page wrote on a button or field.
- **The symbols**: `createBrowserValueSymbols(digest)` numbers values in the order first seen; `digest` is HMAC-SHA256 under
  32 random bytes made for **this** recording (`newRecordingDigest`, `features/browser/recording.ts`), so nothing holds a value
  and two recordings' symbols have nothing to do with each other.
- **The window's one debugger owner** (`browser-window.ts` `createPageDebugger`, `createPageDebuggerOwner` in
  `client/shared/browser-recorder.ts`): the `ai`-mode write gate and a recording are the two holders of the window's ONE
  DevTools connection, never both — `setMode('ai')` does nothing while a recording holds it (the window stays `human`, so
  `run.enterAi` answers false and a run refuses to start), `record.start` answers false while the AI holds it or is about to
  (`state.mode === 'ai'`, a pending attach), and `setMode('human')` never takes a recording's connection away. Only this
  function attaches and detaches; the recording receives observations (`BrowserRecordingObservation`). While recording the
  preview / inspection reads nothing (`preview.read` refuses). Recording attaches `Runtime` (an isolated world
  `acb-recording` with the binding `__acbRecord`, installed only there: `Runtime.addBinding` with `executionContextName`, and
  only a call from that world's context ids counts), `Page` (`addScriptToEvaluateOnNewDocument` with `runImmediately`;
  `frameNavigated` / `navigatedWithinDocument` of the main frame = the page lines; the main frame's id read with `Page.getFrameTree`
  at the start, so a page already loaded that navigates within itself is seen) and `Network` (`maxPostDataSize` 64 KiB;
  `getResponseBody` only for a JSON answer ≤ 1 MiB, its text handed to the recorder and dropped there). The script
  (`BROWSER_RECORDING_SCRIPT`) hears only **trusted** clicks on buttons / choices / links and `change` of fields. **Labels come only from real controls**
  (independent review): a field's own `aria-label`, its `<label>`'s own words (never the field's options or value),
  `placeholder` or `title` — never `aria-labelledby` (text elsewhere on the page); a native button's own text only when it
  holds ≤ 8 elements; anything else acting as a button or a choice only its `aria-label` / `title`, and nothing from a row,
  cell, list item, option or menu item or an element holding many others (a page makes whole rows buttons) (never a
  password's or a file's value; hidden fields ignored). The person's own requests go out as in `human` mode (it is their
  work). The window's title says 「（記録中）」. A first page not loaded within 20 s, the connection lost (`detach`) or the
  window closed end the recording (`ended('closed' | 'lost')`).
- **In main** (`features/browser/recording.ts`, no Electron): one recording per member × company, **in memory only**; starting
  needs the site in the member's current policy (`recordWindow`, the controller) and this PC allowed (`mayRecord` = the same
  switch as the AI's: an enabled registration, or the owner's opt-in) — the server decides again on ［送る］. A start that
  fails, the window closing, the connection lost, ［捨てる］ and sign-out **discard** it (nothing kept, nothing sent; one
  discarded or stopped while still starting lets the window go once the start finishes); stopping stops observing; before
  sending the person **［消す］ a line** (every reference to it cleared, `browserRecordingWithout`), **［伏せる］ a word everywhere**
  — a label, an address segment or query key, a part of a field name or answer key, replaced by `{x}` wherever it stands
  whole (`browserRecordingHideable` lists them, `browserRecordingHide`) — and **removes another host**
  (`browserRecordingWithoutHost`); ［送る］ sends exactly what is left. A refusal or no answer keeps it for another try. IPC `browserRecording:get` / `start` / `stop` / `edit` (`{ kind: line | word | host }`) /
  `send` / `discard` (`recording-ipc.ts`, main window's main frame only; the host only a name); a recording discarded by
  itself sends `browser:changed` so the screen asks again. **This PC's key goes only on the send** (`X-ACB-Browser-Device`,
  `createBrowserRecordingsHttp`).
- **The screen** (`src/features/settings/components/BrowserRecordingSection.tsx`, `hooks/useBrowserRecording.ts`; words
  `BROWSER_RECORDING_APP_WORDS`, button words only — the sentences come from main in the view's `notes` / `titles`, which keeps
  the renderer bundle within its budget): 「記録する：<site>」 per site with what is and is not recorded; while recording the
  line count, ［記録を止める］ ［捨てる］; then 「送る前に確かめてください」 with, first, **the warning that names of customers
  may appear and how to hide them**, each line (ページ / 押した / 入力した / 送った, the core's `browserRecordingLine`,
  `（推定）…` under it) with ［消す］, 「伏せる言葉」 (each hideable word, one press hides it everywhere), the other hosts with
  ［消す］, ［送る］ ［捨てる］ and that it is seen only by the sender and the company's owners / admins and goes in 30 days, and
  that the sender's own consultation AI reads it to make a Skill's draft (other people's AI does not).
- **The server** (`routes/browser-recordings.ts` → `services/browser-recordings.ts` → the handle's `browserRecordings`,
  `ports/browser-recordings.ts`, `adapters/sqlite/browser-recordings.ts`, table `browser_recordings` in
  `db/schema/browser-recordings.ts`, **new table only**: company, `br_` + 32 hex, sender, domain, lines, the content as JSON,
  `created_at`, `delete_at` = +30 days; a trigger refuses any change; shapes `shared/types/browser-recordings.ts`):
  `POST /browser-recordings { domain, content }` + this PC's key header — this PC first (`devices.check`, the same first
  check as every plan step: an enabled registration, or the owner's transition exception), then the body, the member's
  setting 「使う」 with that domain registered (`BROWSER_NOT_ALLOWED`), and **the content exactly as the core writes it**
  (`browserRecordingProblem`: format 1 — an app from before — or 2, whose every send carries `bareQuery`, true only beside
  a query; every object exactly its keys, ids increasing, references only to earlier steps of the right
  kind, every address / label / key already in its kept form — so no digit run, `%`, `@`, query value or token rides in one —,
  every value a mark, no sensitive field, a password field only `secret`, other hosts valid and not the domain, ≤ 500 steps,
  ≤ 512 KiB; `BROWSER_RECORDING_BAD`, `BROWSER_RECORDING_TOO_LARGE`), and **at most 20 kept per member and company**
  (`BROWSER_RECORDING_PER_MEMBER`, 409 `BROWSER_RECORDING_LIMIT` in plain Japanese; one deleted or past its 30 days frees a
  place). **Not in a company archive** (`ARCHIVE_EXCLUDED_TABLES`: temporary, read by the sender and that time's admins). `GET /browser-recordings` (an owner / admin every one of
  the company; anyone else their own), `GET|DELETE /browser-recordings/:recordingId`. **Who reads** (D2, checked on every read
  and delete): the sender — only what they sent while a member this time (`created_at` ≥ their membership's: someone who left
  and came back sees nothing of before) — and current owners / admins (a role taken away counts at once); anyone else, another
  company's and one past `delete_at` get the same 404 `BROWSER_RECORDING_NOT_FOUND`. The sender leaving keeps it for the
  company until its 30 days. **Deleting** (the sender or an owner / admin) removes the row, content included. **30 days**:
  every read leaves out a row past `delete_at`, and the server clock deletes them (`expireBrowserRecordings`, every company,
  `ClockRun.recordingsDeleted`). **Who may record** is the AI's own rule ("Who may use a site"): `siteGrants.allows(member, domain, 'read')` — the company's 「使う」 with the domain registered and this person at least 見るだけ there (使わない is refused, owners and admins follow the same table).
- **The admin console**: 「ブラウザ操作の記録」 (`…/advanced/ai-safety/browser/recordings`, linked from 「ブラウザ操作」 with the PCs and
  people pages; owner/admin only, a member or a department manager sees 「このページはオーナーと管理者だけが使えます」 and nothing
  is asked; words `admin/src/lib/agent-browser-recordings.ts`): who sent it, when, the site, lines, when it goes; ［開く］ shows the
  steps in the core's words and the other hosts; ［消す］ behind a confirm.
- **Remaining limits** (said): a lowercase name (`/users/tanaka`) passes the word rule — the person hides it before
  sending; a label is what the page wrote on a button or field and may name a person; the symbols show a
  flow, not anonymity; `推定` links are by time and equal values only; a page's own worker and an out-of-process frame are not
  observed (their requests are not lines); a large body (> 64 KiB) or an upload has no field names (`null`).

Tests: `client/tests/browser-recorder.test.ts` (a fictional site's run with a password, Cookie, Authorization, CSRF, session,
a customer's name, e-mail and phone, a link text, an answer's values: none of them nor a fragment in the recording's JSON; what
is kept, line by line; the same value the same symbol and two recordings unrelated; a token field masked to `{x}` still
dropped; an upload, a raw body, a GET counted; deleting lines leaves nothing dangling and passes the server's check; the
debugger's one holder), `client/tests/browser-recording-app.test.ts` (a start that fails, a window closed or the connection
lost discard it; no PC, another site or a second recording refused; nothing sent before ［送る］, deleted lines never sent, a
refusal kept; sign-out; the key only on the send), `client/tests/browser-window-electron.test.ts` (a real window: trusted typing
and clicks recorded, the page's forged binding call and synthetic click not; no value, password, CSRF in header or body,
Authorization, link text; the AI cannot enter while it records and no recording starts while the AI holds it; the preview
reads nothing; a closed window ends it as `closed`), `client/tests/ui/browser-settings.spec.ts` (start, stop, delete a line,
only the rest sent), `server/tests/unit/browser-recordings.test.ts` (the server's check refuses a value, a password field, a
CSRF / session field, ids, query values, encoded names, tokens, a forged spelling around placeholders, link texts, labels with
an e-mail or digits, unmasked answer keys, dangling references, extra keys; sending needs the PC, 「使う」, the domain and the person at least 見るだけ (使わない refused); the
sender and owners / admins read, another member, another company, a demoted admin and a sender who left — also after coming
back — do not; deleting; the 30 days on read and by the clock), `admin/tests/agent-browser-recordings.test.tsx`, permission
matrix rows + `CROSS`, `company-scoped-sql.test.ts`, `jobs-schema.test.ts`, `admin-contract.test.ts`, `browser-device.test.ts`
(the recordings' send names the header). Each guard mutation-checked; kept on purpose: the recorder's second sensitive-name
check after masking (the raw-name check in `browserBodyFields` catches first), the binding's context-id check (the binding
exists only in the isolated world), `onDetach` ending a recording as `closed` (the window's `closed` handler does first).
After the independent review: the word rule (capitals, dots, tokens, digits masked whole; `browser-recorder.test.ts` and the
server's refusal table), `{k}` for keys that are data, every digit of a label, labels only from real controls (a row made a
button, an option and a field named by `aria-labelledby` keep none — the real-Electron test), hiding a word and removing a
host before sending (`browser-recorder.test.ts`, `browser-recording-app.test.ts`, `browser-settings.spec.ts`), the cap of 20
(`browser-recordings.test.ts`) and the archive (`company-archive.test.ts`) — each mutation-checked.

## A Skill's browser declaration and its approval (「Skill の承認」, step ⑥ of the redesign, part 1)

Owner decisions 2026-11-25 (internal design memo 「ブラウザ操作：記録から自動化へ」 §3 I3–I5, §5, §6-3, §10 「Skill の承認」, §11
`browser_skill_approvals`, §12 row ⑥, D1, D5, and the owner's additions of that day): the FDE turns a recording into a
**company Skill** (`.claude/skills/<name>/` in the shared folder, or the company's Skill shelf without one) with a small
declaration the core reads, `browser.yaml`; the AI may write only within an **approved version** of it, and **the server
decides** what is approved and builds the write frames itself. Step ⑤ (writes) consumes `approvedFrames`; nothing writes
with it yet. The company's Skills and the departments' ("Department Skills" below).

- **The declaration** (`shared/core/browser-skill.ts`, pure; parsed with the core's strict YAML subset `yaml-lite.ts` — no
  anchors, aliases, tags, block scalars or duplicate keys —, then checked strictly; knows no site): `domain` (its exact
  spelling, `normalizeBrowserHost(d) === d`; **must be a domain the company registered**, `browserSkillDomainProblem`, judged
  on every read), `writes` (1–16 of `{ method: POST | PUT | PATCH | DELETE, path, max: 1–20, fields? }`; `fields` ≤ 8 body
  fields that must hold exactly these values — `_method` only on a POST and only PUT / PATCH / DELETE (I4), **never a login,
  CSRF or session field** (`isSensitiveFieldName`), plain values; the same write twice refused), `never_read` (≤ 32 path
  patterns, a query `key` / `key=value` allowed: a GET that changes something), `after_read` (≤ 16, `{created}` allowed),
  `account_marker` (`{ meta: <name> }`, a generic `<meta name>`), `safe_pattern` (`none` only); a write's optional `id` and
  a later write's `{made:<id>}` ("Writes only to what this run made"). Every object exactly its
  keys, ≤ 8 KiB (UTF-8). **Path patterns** share the recording's placeholders (`{n}`, `{x}`): `/` + plain ASCII segments
  (`A–Z a–z 0–9 . _ ~ -`, never `.` / `..`, no `%`, no empty segment but a trailing `/`), no query in a write's or an
  after-read's path — a write declares its exact query apart, as `query` ("A write with an exact query"). Problems are fixed words (`BROWSER_SKILL_PROBLEM_WORDS`), never a piece of the file. `browserSkillFrames` = a
  frozen deep copy; `browserSkillWriteLine` = a target in plain words for the admin.
- **The server reads its own copy** (`ports/browser-skills.ts` `BrowserSkillSource`, `adapters/browser-skill-source.ts`): the
  company repository's shared branch (async git: `ls-tree`, `cat-file -s` first — over 8 KiB is never read whole) or the shelf's
  current version. **No route or service takes Skill content from an app.** The approval key is `sha256:` of `browser.yaml`;
  the whole folder's fingerprint (`git:<tree id>` / `shelf:<version>`) tells that the other files changed.
- **Table** `browser_skill_approvals` (additive, `db/schema/browser-skills.ts`, behind the handle's `browserSkills`): company,
  `skill_path`, `declaration_hash`, `mode` (`per_run` | `pre_approved`), `how` (`approved` | `edited`), `approved_by`,
  `approved_at`, `files_seen`, `revoked_at` / `revoked_by`; a trigger fixes everything (`seq` included) but `files_seen` and a revoke set once.
  **Only a skill's latest row counts, and only for its hash** (approving another version supersedes; a declaration put back
  to an older text needs approving again).
- **Who changed it** (owner decision): re-approval is needed only when `browser.yaml` changes. **An owner / admin's change
  counts as approved** (`how: edited`, `approved_by` = who; the mode of the approval before is kept, `per_run` when none;
  `files_seen` stays null, so **its first run shows the card once** even when pre-approved — the change may have been
  written by the admin's AI, independent review) — **only when that change touched `browser.yaml` itself** (`declarationChanged`:
  the blob at the push's old and new commit, or the shelf's version before and the one written; an owner / admin who changed
  only the text of a Skill whose declaration someone else changed approves nothing, Codex review), **only while it is still
  what the server holds** (each landing is compared with a fresh read right before it writes: a push or publish read in
  another order, or an approval made meanwhile, is never superseded by an older landing, Codex review), and **only on top of
  an approved declaration** — the one right before the change (`previousDeclaration`: the blob at the push's old commit, or
  the shelf's version before) must be the latest approval's, in force (not revoked), or there was none and nothing was ever
  approved (a new Skill); a skill editor's pending lines that a `pull --rebase` carried into an owner's push wait
  (independent review M1). On the shelf only the publisher's **own upload** counts: putting an earlier version back
  (`restore`) or publishing a member's request (「公開する」) waits for 「Skill の承認」 (review M2). Anyone else's change (「Skill を直せる人」) waits, and the other
  owners / admins are told once per version; **listing 「Skill の承認」 also tells of any waiting version nobody was told of**
  (a landing skipped, past the push's limit, a server-made commit; the notice's key keeps it once — review L4). A declaration that does not parse is never approved. **Whose change**: for a push, the pusher git-http authenticated and the role it read
  (`routes/git-http.ts`, company repository only) — the push checker writes the `<old> <new> <ref>` lines it **accepted** to
  `ACB_PUSH_NOTES` (a file in the request's private temp dir, `preparePushCheck`; never on a refusal; a failure to write only
  means "not counted as approved"); after the push succeeded `services.browserSkills.afterPush` reads, off the request, each
  touched company Skill **at the pushed commit** (`BrowserSkillSource.pushed`: never the tip, which may already be someone
  else's). For the shelf, the publisher (`SkillShelfDeps.changed`, after a publish, a restore or a request published, with the
  version before and **exactly the version written**, `BrowserSkillSource.shelfLanded`).
- **Only the other files changed** (the steps' text, site notes): nothing is blocked; `approvedFrames` answers
  `textChanged` / `needsRunCard: true` (also when pre-approved) until step ⑤ calls `runCardShown(skill, hash, files)`
  once the per-run card was shown; owners / admins (but the person who changed it) are told 「手順が直りました」 once per folder
  version. An explicit approval counts the files **as the page showed them** as seen (the approval sends the folder's `files`
  back with the hash; either one moved → 409 `BROWSER_SKILL_CHANGED`).
- **For step ⑤**: `runCardShown`'s `files` must be the value its own `approvedFrames` call returned, never one the app sends
  (else an app could switch the card off); `approvedFrames` answers about the approval only — the permit still asks the
  company's mode, the site, the person's level on it, this PC and the stops. Since ⑤a ("Writes within frames") the
  frames service does exactly that, and asks `stillApproved(member, skill, hash, approval)` (synchronous, no git read;
  `approval` = the row `approvedFrames` answered) inside each transaction after its `approvedFrames` read, so an approval
  revoked, superseded or given another mode in between (a `pre_approved` turned `per_run`) counts.
- **`approvedFrames(member, skill)`** (step ⑤'s question at every permit; a few git reads): the Skill read now, the declaration
  valid (`BROWSER_SKILL_BAD_DECLARATION`), its domain registered now (`BROWSER_SKILL_DOMAIN_NOT_REGISTERED`), the latest
  approval for this very hash and not revoked (`BROWSER_SKILL_NOT_APPROVED`), else `BROWSER_SKILL_NOT_FOUND` → `{ frames,
  declarationHash, files, mode, textChanged, needsRunCard }`. No route: the permit is the server's.
- **Routes** (`routes/browser-skills.ts`, owner / admin, else 403 `BROWSER_SKILL_FORBIDDEN`): `GET /browser-skill-approvals`
  (`BrowserSkillsView`, `shared/types/browser-skills.ts`: each Skill with its hash, status `waiting` / `approved` /
  `pre_approved` / `text_changed` / `revoked` / `invalid`, the problem in plain Japanese, domain, write targets in plain words,
  never-read and after-read addresses, account marker, the approval in force; its `path` and `department`), `POST
  /browser-skill-approvals { skill, declarationHash, files, mode }` (`skill`: the Skill's path, a bare name = the company's; exactly these keys; the hash and the folder's version must be the ones read now, else 409 `BROWSER_SKILL_CHANGED`; an invalid
  declaration or an unregistered domain 409), `POST /browser-skill-approvals/revoke { skill }` (the latest approval, 409
  `BROWSER_SKILL_NOT_APPROVED` when none).
- **Notices**: on the existing admin-only browser kind `browser_shape_changed` (recordless, built when read, shown only to
  owners / admins; its label generalised to 「ブラウザ操作で管理者の確かめが要る（サイトの形・Skill。管理者だけ）」) with keys
  `browser_skill:waiting:<name>:<16 hex>` and `browser_skill:text:<name>:<16 hex>` (a department Skill's hex also hashes
  its path: "Department Skills"; composition hands `browser_skill:` keys to
  `browserSkills.noticeText`): a new kind would grow every app's bundle and settings list.
- **Admin** 「Skill の承認」 (`…/advanced/ai-safety/browser/skills`, linked from 「ブラウザ操作」; owner/admin only, a member or
  manager sees 「このページはオーナーと管理者だけが使えます」 and nothing is asked; words `admin/src/lib/agent-browser-skills.ts`, no
  site word): each Skill with its badge by meaning (承認待ち `awaiting`, 承認済み / 前もって承認 `success`, 手順の文が直った `info`,
  取り消し済み `paused`, 使えません `failed`), the targets in plain words, the addresses, who approved (「宣言を直したので承認」 for
  `edited`); ［承認する］ / ［前もって承認］ send the version shown; ［取り消す］ behind a confirm; nothing for an invalid one.
  Each row says where the Skill belongs (「全社」 / 「部署「<folder>」」, `skillPlaceWords`) and sends its `path`.

### Department Skills (owner bug report 2026-11-27: most companies keep their Skills per department)

- **Where** (`shared/core/browser-skill.ts` `parseBrowserSkillPath`, `BrowserSkillPlace`): the company's
  `.claude/skills/<name>` and a department folder's `<department>/.claude/skills/<name>` — exactly where Claude Code reads
  Skills for a consultation (its working folder is the company folder or a department's, `settingSources: ['project']`
  reads `.claude/skills/` there and in its parents) and where the shelf places a department's Skill (`shelfTargets`;
  also a set watch's `department_skill`, `jobs.md`). One department folder by the department folder name rule, NFC, a
  valid Skill name; nothing deeper (`<dept>/x/.claude/skills/…`: Claude Code does not load it at the start of a
  consultation — decision for the owner), no `.agents/skills/` (Codex's twin; the declaration is read from
  `.claude/skills/` only, as before). The git source finds them with one `ls-tree -d` of the shared branch's top folders
  and one `ls-tree -r` / `diff-tree` over `.claude/skills/` and each `<folder>/.claude/skills/` (async, ≤ 500 folders,
  ≤ 100 Skills); the shelf source takes items with a department too (`<department folder as stored>/.claude/skills/<name>`;
  a department gone → none). **Whether every place was looked into** (`BrowserSkillPaths.complete`): false with more
  than 500 top folders (exactly 500 is complete), more than 100 Skills on the list, git failing, or a top folder that
  would be a department folder once in NFC but is not written in NFC (a Mac commit without precomposed Unicode: not looked
  into). The admin page says so above the list (`BrowserSkillsView.complete`, `SKILLS_INCOMPLETE`).
- **Never a private folder's**: its files are in its own scope repository, which nothing here reads; and a path at or
  under a private folder's path (`privateFolders.paths()`, compared by `pathKey`) is left out of `list`, `read` and
  `pushed` even if the company repository held one, so its name is in no answer (decision on the safe side: a Skill
  inside a private folder is out of scope).
- **A Skill is its path**: `browser_skill_approvals.skill_path`, the frames run's `skill`, the source's
  `BrowserSkillContent.path`, the admin's `BrowserSkillView.path` (+ `department`) and the approve / revoke body's `skill`.
  **A bare name means the company's** (`browserSkillPlaceOf`): rows written before (`.claude/skills/<name>`), a run started
  before, an older admin page — all keep their meaning. Two departments' Skills of one name are two Skills: approving,
  revoking, the frames and the notices never cross (a department Skill's notice key is
  `browser_skill:waiting:<department>/<name>:<16 hex>`, its hex also hashing the path; the company's keeps
  `browser_skill:waiting:<name>:<16 hex>`; the text says where: 「部署「営業」の Skill「…」」 / 「全社の Skill「…」」,
  `browserSkillPlaceWords`).
  Renaming a department moves its Skills' paths, so they need approving again (safe side).
- **The AI names it by name or path** (`browser_work`'s `skill`, `browserSkillRef`): the app sends it as given (a path
  in NFC); the server's `resolve` takes a name only when exactly **one** Skill folder of that name is there **for this
  member** (the company's, or a department's they read) — **with or without `browser.yaml`** (the AI sees a SKILL.md-only
  Skill too: a company `.claude/skills/coupons` without a declaration never lets `coupons` mean a department's, review of
  #879) — two → 409 `BROWSER_SKILL_AMBIGUOUS` (the AI gets
  `BROWSER_WORK_WORDS.skillAmbiguous`: give the path, or ask the person), none → `BROWSER_SKILL_NOT_FOUND`. Never a
  guess, never a preference for the department on screen. The name is looked up in every place (`BrowserSkillSource.withName`,
  not the admin list's bound of 100: a second Skill past it would be guessed away — Codex review); when some place could
  not be looked into (`complete` false, above) even one match is refused the same way. An older app that sends
  a bare name meant for the company's Skill is now refused while a department the member reads has one of that name too
  (its words: the generic 「使えません」; safe side). The run keeps the resolved path, and the app reads the
  operator's steps (SKILL.md, site-notes.md) from **that** path in the member's company folder
  (`features/browser/skill-steps.ts`; after the frames run started, never from the AI's word), and hands them over only when
  they are exactly the text the server sealed for that folder version ("The operator works from the approved steps").
- **Who uses it**: a department's Skill only by a member who **reads its path now** (`BrowserSkillDeps.readsPath`,
  composition: `resolveAccess` over the company's scopes — read-restricted places of a company without the shared
  folder, private folders' gates), asked by `resolve`, by `approvedFrames` at every start, approve and permit (else the
  same `BROWSER_SKILL_NOT_FOUND` as a missing one, and a permit's refusal ends the run) and by `stillApproved` inside
  the permit's transaction. The company's Skills are used as before (no new check). Approving and revoking stay owner /
  admin. **Whose change**: unchanged — the pusher's company role as git-http authenticated it, or the shelf publisher's;
  a department's 部署の管理役 or 「Skill を直せる人」 is not an owner / admin, so their change to a department Skill's
  declaration waits like any other.
- **A declaration new at its path** (a new Skill, or one an owner / admin moved or copied there) counts as their approval
  only when it is nobody's pending or taken-back declaration (review of #879; `newPathApprovable`): every other Skill path
  that held exactly the same `browser.yaml` right before or right after the change (`BrowserSkillLanded.sameElsewhere`:
  the push's old and new commits by blob id, `sameBlobAt`; on the shelf the other items' current versions) has it approved
  and in force, every place could be looked into, and no approval of that hash was ever revoked in the company
  (`BrowserSkillApprovalRepository.everRevoked`). Else it waits like a skill editor's change. So moving or copying a
  pending declaration, or putting a revoked one back at another path, approves nothing; copying an approved one (every
  copy approved) does.

Tests: `server/tests/unit/browser-skill-core.test.ts` (the shape, unknown keys, sizes in UTF-8 bytes, YAML anchors and
duplicate keys, the path grammar, `_method`, sensitive fields, duplicates, the domain, the frozen frames, the plain words),
`server/tests/unit/browser-skills.test.ts` (no frames until approved, exactly the version seen, pre-approved, owners / admins
only, the strict body, revoked and an edit after it, only the latest counts, another company's approval, an unregistered
domain, a bad declaration, an owner / admin's change approved with who and the mode kept and the card once, only a change of the
declaration itself approving, a landing no longer current changing nothing, an editor's change waits and owners /
admins told — not the editor, once —, an unreadable one never approved, the text change: frames kept, the card once even
pre-approved, told once, `runCardShown`, an admin's own text change, the server's copy only, the trigger),
`server/tests/integration/browser-skill-push.test.ts` (real git-http, checker and source: the checker hands over only what it
accepted; an owner's push approved, an editor's waits, a refused push changes nothing, a text-only push, an owner's text-only push
after an editor's declaration approving nothing, a push read at its own commit; the shelf's publisher), `admin/tests/agent-browser-skills.test.tsx`, `admin-contract.test.ts`, permission matrix rows,
`company-scoped-sql.test.ts`, `jobs-schema.test.ts`, `client/tests/browser-core-words.test.ts` (the new files scanned).
Department Skills: `browser-skill-core.test.ts` (where a Skill stands, the AI's name or path), `browser-skills.test.ts`
"department Skills" (listed with the department and approved by path; two departments of one name kept apart — approval,
revoke, notices —; a name refused when two Skills share it, candidates only what the member reads; a member who cannot
read the department, also mid-run (`stillApproved`); an old `.claude/skills/<name>` row by name and path; an owner's own
edit approves, an editor's waits; bad paths), `browser-frames.test.ts` "a department Skill" (the run keeps the resolved
path, a shared name refused, a member losing the department mid-run gets no permit — before and inside the
transaction), `browser-skill-push.test.ts` "department Skills" (real git: an owner's department push approved and
listed, a deeper one never listed, an editor's waits, approval by path; a private folder's path never listed or read,
its name in no answer; the shelf's department item and a read-restricted department), `client/tests/browser-frame-writes.test.ts`
(the AI's name or NFC path sent as given, the steps read from the server's path, `skillAmbiguous`, bad references refused
before anything; `readBrowserSkillSteps` reads only the Skill's own folder), `admin/tests/agent-browser-skills.test.tsx`
(two departments' rows, each approving by its path; the incomplete list said). The review of #879: `browser-skills.test.ts` "department Skills: the review of #879" (a SKILL.md-only company Skill makes a name ambiguous; the list's `complete`; a pending declaration moved or copied and a revoked one put back wait, an approved one copied is approved; the notice's department), `browser-skill-push.test.ts` (real git: a move and a copy of a pending declaration, a copy of an approved one; a SKILL.md-only company folder; exactly 500 top folders complete, 501 not; a non-NFC top folder makes the answer incomplete).
Each guard mutation-checked.

## The operator's window tools (step ④ of the redesign: the operator reads a registered domain freely; writes stay refused)

Owner-approved design memo 「ブラウザ操作：記録から自動化へ／サイトの言葉を本体から外す」 (2026-11-25) §3 I1 / I2 / I5 / I6 / I8 /
I10, §6-1 steps 1–2, §7-1 (the operator, the lock), §9 bug 3, §12 row ④, D6, D10. Inside a domain the company registered,
the operator AI may move through pages freely **reading only**, without per-site code: the consultation AI hands it an
instruction in words (「この一覧の受付の終わりを並べて」); writes stay refused by the window's gate (step ⑤ adds them). Behind the
same switch as everything else (`BROWSER_AI_BUILD_SWITCH` / this PC's registration or opt-in).

- **Pieces.** `client/shared/browser-browse.ts` (pure: the limits `BROWSER_BROWSE_LIMITS` — the `standard` budget's 20 steps and 5 minutes ("Room for a Skill run"), 90 s each
  in all, a page's text ≤ 30 000 characters, ≤ 400 elements, in priority order ("The operator sees the screen") —, the snapshot `BrowserPageSnapshot` and its check
  `readBrowserPageSnapshot`, the actions, `parseBrowseAnswer`, the operator's fixed prompt `BROWSER_OPERATOR_BROWSE_PROMPT`,
  `browseMaterial`, `browseLogStep`, the consultation AI's words `BROWSER_BROWSE_WORDS` / `browseWordsForAi`, the member's card
  `browseCard`, the window port `BrowserBrowseSurface`); `client/shared/browser-browse-script.ts` (the page scripts, plain
  strings); the shell's `browse` surface (`electron/browser-window.ts`); `electron/features/browser/browse.ts`
  (`wireBrowserBrowse`: the loop, the cards in memory; no Electron); the tool `browser_browse` (`electron/browser-request/
  inspect-tool.ts`); server `shared/core/browser-run-log.ts`, `routes/browser-runs.ts` → `services/browser-runs.ts` → the
  handle's `browserRuns` (`ports/browser-runs.ts`, `adapters/sqlite/browser-runs.ts`, `db/schema/browser-runs.ts`), API shapes
  `shared/types/browser-runs.ts`.
- **The operator** is the closed start of "The operator and the preview", unchanged — `--tools ""`, no MCP, no settings,
  `--max-turns 1`, every init check — with only its system prompt chosen from fixed ones (`browserOperatorArgs(model,
  'browse', toolset)`, a closed choice: nothing else can be passed). It has **no tool**: its "window tools" are its answer, exactly
  one JSON object per step — in today's tool set (every other tool set's words are read into these: "The operator's tool sets") `click` / `type` / `select` (an element `ref` of the page it was just shown, of a fitting role:
  typing only into a text field, never a password field; an option the select showed; since "Pressing by position" also a
  press at a point of the screen image and typing into the focused field), `go` (a path of the domain: starts
  with `/`, no `//`, `\`, `#`, space or control), `back`, `wait` (1–5 s), `done { result, unread }` or `stop { why:
  not-found | need-person | blocked, unread }`. Anything else ends the run (`bad-answer`), nothing done. Any answer may
  carry `notes` (≤ 4000; 2000 until "The operator sees the screen"): the operator's own working memory, handed back to it at the next step (each step is a fresh
  process; Codex review), and `reason` (its short reason for the answer); both reach the member's card only, never the
  consultation AI ("The operator's trail on the card").
- **Main acts** in the member's own window (controller `browseWindow`): `enter` puts it in `ai` mode with a decider that
  **refuses every write** (`decide: () => false`; reads pass, the request rule and frames as in any `ai` run) and dialogs
  dismissed, never accepted (`dismissDialogs`: `Page.handleJavaScriptDialog { accept: false }`, so a confirm never says yes
  and a page cannot hold the window; each alert / confirm / prompt is told to the operator with its text, "What a press
  did"). The page is read and acted on from an **isolated world** (`BROWSER_BROWSE_WORLD`,
  `executeJavaScriptInIsolatedWorld`: the page's script cannot see the element list or replace the functions used): the
  snapshot is the address, title, visible text (`innerText`) and the visible usable elements — a text field named by its
  label / placeholder only, **a password field's value never read**, a link's path only when it stays on the domain —,
  and, as role `item`, elements that act as controls without being one, each with its box on the screen ("The operator
  sees the screen"). Presses
  are the window's own trusted input (`sendInputEvent`, at the element's centre after two drawn frames) and typing goes
