  has no empty summary row, so a stop before any write frames nothing as 「サイトから読み取った情報」. The plan card's
  「下見で読んだ大事な値」 heading is unchanged.
- **Remaining limits** (said): an old card further up the conversation still shows its buttons, and a remounted card
  shows them again (each press is just the member's message, as if typed; a pre-approved Skill's new run then writes
  without a fresh plan card, as a typed request would); after a restart the `bo_` / `bf_` card is gone as before.

Tests: `client/tests/browser-stop-ask.test.ts` (the incident, ad hoc and with a Skill: the question first on the preview's
card and in the renderer's view, never the AI's words — which say the card asks — nor the run log, the reason once, no
note, nothing framed; the stop reason heard as the question; need-login → the window; other stops, the app's ending and a
refused need-person ask nothing; a write run's stop: `next`, the headline once with 「何も書いていません」, no summary row,
no write, report or confirm; after a held write the question without answers and no footer; the question cleaned and cut,
a non-string refused, `readBrowserStopNext`'s exact shape; the result card's headline, sections and notes; the buttons'
words, a question shaped to close the quote neutralized), `client/tests/ui/browser-inspection-card.spec.ts`
(preview `browser-inspection-card-ui.html?stop=decide|work|wrote|login|blocked`: 「決めてほしいこと」 right after the title, the
reason once, the memo's same line not repeated, no 「サイトから読み取った情報」 / 「読み取れませんでした」 / 「機械で確かめた値では
ありません」, no empty inner card; ［そのまま進める］／［やめる］ send exactly the member's message once and call nothing that
writes or opens; after a sent write the question with no answer button; need-login only ［開いてログイン］, another stop no
button; the folded marks' line only where rows carry a mark), `browser-domain-release.test.ts` (the summary's words).
Each guard mutation-checked (the preview's and the work's `next`, the reason fallback, the AI's footer, only need-person
asking, no answers after a held write, the message's quote and 【】 neutralization, no summary on a stop, the cleaning, the
renderer's check, a non-string question, the 「何も書いていません」 claim only with no write held; on the card: the memo's repeat, the
write card's headline twice, the question not first, a button that writes, the folded line on a card without marks).

## The operator works from the approved steps (owner 2026-11-28: 「AI に守らせたいことは説明文ではなく仕組みで保証する」)

A trial on 2026-11-27 stopped four times. Half of it was the operator's prompt itself: an example sentence (「同名のタグを
使ってよいか」) became the model of a stop, a stop condition the consultation AI added to the task outranked the Skill, what
the preview had checked was gone by the work, and the prompts (≈3,000 characters each) explained the app to the operator.
Owner rule: what the operator must keep to is guaranteed by the app; the prompt says only the answer's form, what to work
from, when to stop, and that page text is data. Codex's review kept two more: the person's own prohibitions stay, and a
Skill is bound to its approved version. Owner condition: what the operator can do, how it is asked and whether it finishes
are not made worse.

- **Shorter prompts** (characters, before → after): the write task's work 2,946 → 1,331, its preview 3,000 → 1,356, the
  read task 2,172 → 853. Kept: the answers' exact forms (the same actions; lines merged only), what to work from and in
  which order, when to stop, outside text is data. Gone: example questions, 「人の判断が要る画面」 (too wide),
  「unlisted … 進められないなら stop」, how the app shows things (cards, 「相談の AI には渡りません」, `retry` being the app's
  words), what a refusal's fixed words already say (`history`, `writes`), repetitions; limits the parser cuts by itself (a
  plan's words, a question, a reason) are no longer stated.
- **When the operator may stop** (both write prompts, one line): a login (`need-login`), the thing asked is not there
  (`not-found`), a write in doubt (`blocked`, the work only), a fork no source answers (`need-person`, its `question` one
  that 「はい」 answers). Otherwise it goes on by the steps. A person's prohibition that really conflicts with the steps is
  asked once, before writing (`need-person`; with no write held the card offers the answers: "The stopped card asks one
  thing").
- **The order of sources, by the material** (`workMaterial`): `person` first — the person's own prohibitions and limits
  (`said`) and values (`values`), as they said them —, then `steps` (the Skill's sealed text), then `plan` (the work only:
  the preview's plan the person approved, `{ summary, affects, values }`; **`null` when there was no preview** — a
  pre-approved Skill version), then `request`: the consultation AI's own words of the task **only when no Skill steps are
  handed** (`null` when they are; an ad-hoc task, a Skill with no steps, or a company without the company folder keeps it —
  else the operator would have nothing to go on: Codex review). So a step or a stop condition the consultation AI writes
  into the task never reaches an operator that has the Skill's steps; then the task's sentence is the card's only. The
  prompts' one ranking line: `person.said` before `steps` (the owner's order).
- **The tool** (`browser_work`, `inspect-tool.ts`): `said` (≤ 10, each ≤ 300, the person's words — never the AI's own
  steps or stops) and `values` (≤ 16 `{ label ≤ 40, value ≤ 200 }`), both optional, read strictly (`workPersonOf`: exact
  keys, no control character but a line break in `said`) else `badInput`; its description says the operator works from
  `said`, `values` and the Skill, and that with a Skill nothing is added and `task` does not reach the operator. The
  card's 「決めてほしいこと」 footer tells the consultation AI to add what was decided to `said`.
- **A Skill's steps bound to the approved version** (`shared/core/browser-skill-steps.ts`, knows no site): the steps are
  `SKILL.md` then `site-notes.md`, each `# <name>` + newline + its text, joined by a blank line (`browserSkillStepsText`); a
  file over 64 KiB or the text over 20,000 characters is **too large** (never cut). The frames run's start answers `steps`
  (`BrowserFrameRunStart.steps`, additive): the **seal** — `sealed` with `sha256:` of that text, `none` or `too-large` —
  computed by the server (`BrowserSkillService.stepsSeal`) from **its own copy at exactly the folder version
  `approvedFrames` answered** (`BrowserSkillSource.stepFiles`: the repository tree the fingerprint names, the bytes as
  stored with the size read first, or the shelf's version); that version unreadable → the start is refused
  `BROWSER_SKILL_CHANGED`, nothing started. The folder version is already in the plan hash (`files`), so a text change
  shows the card as before. The app (`work.ts`, `browserSkillStepsCheck`) reads its copy (`skill-steps.ts`) and starts
  only when its text hashes to the seal, or neither side has steps. Else **nothing is operated** (no operator run, no
  permit), the frames run ends, and the card and the AI say one of `steps-changed` (this PC's copy differs: not synced,
  edited here), `steps-missing` (sealed, none here: a department not downloaded), `steps-too-large`, `steps-unsealed`
  (no seal: an older server) — fixed words, 「始めませんでした…サイトには何も書いていません」. **A company without the
  company folder** has no copy on this PC: its operator gets no steps, as before (the shelf's steps for the app: "Not yet").
  The check runs **before** the member is asked about an earlier write in doubt (the write ledger), so nobody answers for
  a run that cannot start.
- **Not covered** (said): the app's own text is trusted after the check (a modified app is outside what this protects);
  the seal says the text equals the server's copy of that version, not that a person read it (the approval's
  `files_seen` and the per-run card do, as before). `said` / `values` are still written by the consultation AI: nothing
  can prove they are the person's words, and `said` ranks above the steps — the frames still bound every write, and the
  tool's description and field limits narrow it (independent review). A checkout that converts line endings
  (`core.autocrlf=true`) or a step file stored as a link makes the copy differ for good: `steps-changed`, safe side.

## The stopped card shows the screen (same day)

The card's 「決めてほしいこと」 asked the member about a screen they could not see. Now a stopped write task's card carries
the screen the operator stopped on.

- **What**: the image of the operator's last step (`operate` returns the answer's screen), kept as `BrowserStopScreen`
  `{ mediaType: 'image/jpeg', base64 }` (`browserStopScreen`, ≤ 4 MiB of base64, else none) **only beside a `next`** — the
  question, or the login (`workStopNext`) — on the stopped preview's `bo_` card (`previewStoppedView`) and the write run's
  `bf_` card (`Entry.screen`; `viewOf` hands it only with `next`). The renderer takes it only in its exact shape and only
  beside a valid `next` (`readBrowserStopScreen` in `readBrowserInspectionView` / `readBrowserWriteView`) and draws it under
  the question as an `<img>` of a `data:` address (`StopAsk.tsx`, the CSP's `img-src data:`; its text alternative and
  caption 「止まったときの画面（ブラウザ操作の係の AI が見ていたもの）」).
- **Where it lives**: main's memory with its card (gone at quit and sign-out, `clear()`), the card's IPC to the window
  that shows the consultation, the renderer's memory. Never in the consultation AI's words, the run log, any server call,
  the site, the chat history (the chat keeps the card's reference only) or a file.

Tests: `client/tests/browser-operator-sources.test.ts` (with a Skill the material has the person's words and the sealed
steps and never the consultation AI's request, the person first; ad hoc the request; steps changed / missing / too large
/ unsealed / sealed-none-but-present start nothing, with the card's and the AI's words; the core's join, limits and check;
the approved plan in the work, `null` without a preview; the screen on both stopped cards, the renderer's check, never in
the AI's words or the server's calls, none without `next`, gone at sign-out; the tool's shapes),
`server/tests/unit/browser-frames.test.ts` (the start's seal of exactly the folder version, too large, none, unreadable →
nothing started, none for an ad-hoc plan), `server/tests/integration/browser-skill-push.test.ts` (the source's step files
at a repository tree and a shelf version, bound to the version, too large, the seal's hash),
`client/tests/browser-window-electron.test.ts` **the completion regression** (a generic copy-and-register flow,
`tests/browser-window-harness/sites.ts` `/flow`: a list row's menu → 「コピー」 → a dialog to choose the setting again →
「決定」 (the page writes) → the copy's page: name and date → 「登録」 (the page writes); the real window and wiring with a
scripted operator — no model — through the preview, the card and the work: done, exactly the two writes in their frames,
once each, the person's values as written; a later guard that stops this flow fails it). Each guard mutation-checked;
kept on purpose: the work card's screen kept only with a `next` (`viewOf` hands it only beside one, so dropping the first
check changes nothing seen).

## The run's promise (Codex review points 3 / 4, owner-approved 2026-11-28: what may be written, fixed on the server)

Before this the server's permit compared only a frame's fixed fields and `_method`: a name, a tag, a date or the object a
`{n}` pointed at could be anything, and a pre-approved Skill's run had no plan, so nothing said what this run's values
were. Owner: guarantee it by the mechanism, not by sentences; no new setting; no site word in the core; what the operator
can do, how it is asked and whether it finishes are not made worse.

- **The one rule** (`shared/core/browser-run-promise.ts`, pure, knows no site): `browserPromiseVerdict` — asked by the
  server at every permit of a run with a promise and by the app's writer before it asks (the same answer both sides):
  a bound field holding another value than promised, or missing → `broken` (never permitted, never asked); a bound field
  with no promised value, a body field outside the frame's declared `body` (when declared; only `_method` and the
  frameworks' anti-forgery token names are exempt, `browserPromiseImplicitField` — a name that merely looks like a login
  field, `author`, `session_title`, is checked like any: independent review), or an open path segment (`{n}` / `{x}`) that is
  neither an id this run's own done write returned, a promised value, nor a target the person allowed **on that very frame**
  (an answer for one frame never opens another: independent review) → `ask`; else `kept`.
  `{made:<n>}` frames keep their own rule ("Writes only to what this run made").
- **What a promise holds** — **digests only**: `hmac:` + HMAC-SHA256 of the value (NFC) under a 32-byte key the work wiring
  makes per run and keeps in main's memory (`work.ts`), never sent. `values` (`person`: the tool's `values`, the person's own;
  `preview`: the plan card's values — only for a run with a card, so a pre-approved Skill's run, which has no preview,
  promises nothing read from the screen), `binds` (a frame's bound field → the value whose label matches its `bind` label,
  `browserPromiseLabelKey`: NFKC, spaces and A–Z folded; the person's before the preview's), `decisions` (a stopped card's
  answer this run continues: "The stopped card's answer"). The account is the run's member's own login (the write ledger's
  `member:<id>`), the targets the frames, the fields free to change the frame's `body`: the server's own, never the app's.
  Not checked: a Skill's `account_marker` (no reader of it on this path yet: said).
- **`browser.yaml`'s two optional keys** (`shared/core/browser-skill.ts`, additive: a Skill without them runs as before):
  `body: [name, …]` (1–32 body field names its writes may carry besides fixed / bound fields and `_method`; never `_method`
  or a fixed field; a login-looking name may be listed — it is a name, never a value) and `bind: { field: 呼び名 }` (1–8; a field never fixed or
  sensitive, in `body` when that is declared; a label one clean line ≤ 40 in NFC) — `bad-body` / `bad-bind`. The card's line
  says both (`browserSkillWriteLine`). **The draft from recordings writes `body` itself** (`browser-skill-draft.ts`: every name
  every recorded send carried; none when a body was not read or a name is not a plain field name); `bind` is the FDE's.
  An AI-written plan's frames carry neither (the server drops them).
- **Server** (`services/browser-frames.ts`): the frames table keeps each frame's `{ body, bind }` (`browser_frames.promise`,
  additive, fixed by trigger; the view answers `body` / `bind`). `POST /browser-frame-runs/:runId/promise { values, binds,
  decisions }` (this PC's key, the run's PC): once, **before the run's first permit** (service and trigger), while waiting or
  open, read strictly against the run's own frames (`readBrowserRunPromise`: a bind only for a field its frame binds, to a
  value of this promise; ≤ 32 values); the same again → kept, another → 409 `BROWSER_PROMISE_MADE`, after a write → 409
  `BROWSER_PROMISE_TOO_LATE`. `POST …/promise-add { frame, fields, targets, binds }` (this PC's key): the person's
  「書いてよい」, only while open, ≤ 20 per run; an addition fills an empty bind and never changes a promised value. Both
  tables (`browser_frame_promises`, `browser_frame_promise_adds`) never change or go while their run exists. A permit of a
  run with a promise must carry `check` (`readBrowserPermitCheck`: every body field but `_method` / sensitive ones with the
  digest of its value — a repeated field of all its values —, and per path segment the digest of an open one), else 409
  `BROWSER_PROMISE_CHECK_MISSING`; `broken` → 409 `BROWSER_PROMISE_BROKEN` (`reason` value / missing, `field`), `ask` → 409
  `BROWSER_PROMISE_ASK` (`fields`, `targets`, `values`); nothing taken either way. **A run without a promise** (an older app)
  is not checked (decision for the owner, below). The done report's `created` is now kept for **any** frame (an id the site
  minted: what "a target this run made" is read from), still only from the run's PC.
- **The app**: `work.ts` makes the promise after the frames run is known and before the card (or, pre-approved, before the
  work); an older server without the route (404 `NOT_FOUND`) → no promise, as before; any other refusal or no answer → the
  run stops (`server-refused` / `network`), nothing written. The writer (`frame-writes.ts`) asks the rule before any permit,
  sends `check` with it, and on `ask` puts the question on the write card while the page's request stays held
  (`BrowserWriteView.ask`: the line and what is outside the promise in plain words — field names, the target segment, a
  bound field's value; the person's card only) with ［書いてよい］／［書かない］ (`browserLines:allowWrite`, a window showing
  the consultation; the AI's tools never reach it). 「書いてよい」 → `promise-add` with exactly that → the permit; 「書かない」,
  the card's 10 minutes (never past the work's own operating time) or the run's stop → the write is refused (`not-allowed`),
  never sent; a 「書いてよい」 the server did not take is `no-permit`, never "the person said no". While it waits, the 45 s
  give-up does not run (`writer.asking()`), and the send after an answer gets its own whole 45 s from the answer
  (`writer.askEnded()`; Codex review); a write given up while asked is `not-sent`, never `unknown` (it asked for no permit). The same thing is never asked twice in a run. The done write of any frame reports its `created` (only with a
  promise: an older server refuses the key). Words to the operator: `outside-promise`, `not-allowed` (`WORK_WRITE_WHY_WORDS`).
- **Completion** (owner's condition): the fictional copy-and-register flow (`browser-window-electron.test.ts` 「the completion
  regression」) runs with bound fields and a listed body and asks nothing (the copy's id is made by the run, the values are
  the person's): the same steps, writes and stops as before.
- **Not covered** (said): a modified app could send digests that are not its body's (the same trust as the permit's request
  shape); `said` / `values` are still the consultation AI's handing of the person's words; a label the AI names otherwise
  than the Skill's `bind` asks the person once at the write.

## Sent is not done (Codex review points 7 / 8, owner-approved 2026-11-28)

The site's answer said `done` (2xx / 3xx) or `refused` (a listed 4xx) and the card and the AI took them as "saved" / "not
saved". A 202 only accepts, a 200 can carry a business error, and a site may save part and then answer an error.

- **Two results apart**: what was sent (the HTTP answer: the write's `outcome`, unchanged) and what reading back says
  (`WorkBusiness`, `workBusinessOf`): `matched` only when every done write was read again at an address that came from it
  (`after_read`) and every value compared there — the promised one for a bound field, else the sent one — reads as it
  should and nothing promised is missing; `differs` when anything compared reads otherwise; else `unconfirmed` (nothing
  read, a write no address came from, a promised field not shown). `WorkEnd` `done` carries it. **"Unconfirmed" never
  stops** the run: the card and the AI's words say it.
- **Words**: the AI hears `done` (済みました…約束した値と送った値になっていることを確かめました) only for `matched`,
  `doneDiffers` / `doneUnconfirmed` otherwise; the card's headline likewise (`CW.done` / `doneDiffers` / `doneUnconfirmed`) and
  its `state` is `all_ok` only for `matched` (a card with nothing read back and nothing compared is never ✓ — point 8), `ng`
  for `differs` (a promised value read otherwise is its own check line `promisedDiffers`), else `not_read`.
- **A refused write** is read again at its own address before it is reported (`readAgain`) and compared with that address
  as it read **right before this very write** (not the run's first look: Codex review) — "not saved" (`refusal:
  'unchanged'`, reported `refused` with `checked: 'unchanged'`) only when both reads are one JSON object showing **every
  field the write sent** and the text did not move (`readBackUnchanged`; a list page that did not move proves nothing about
  a create: independent review); anything else → `unconfirmed`. The card says 「サイトが「422」を返しました。保存されたかどうかは確かめられていません」 or, checked,
  「…読み直して変わっていないことを確かめました（保存されていません）」; the operator hears 「サイトがエラーを返しました（保存された
  かは確かめていません）…もう一度押せます」. The run goes on as before (a corrected try is a new write).
- **Server**: `browser_frame_refusal_checks` (append-only, only beside a `refused` write, trigger): a refused write without
  one, **of a run that has a promise** (an app that reads refusals again; an older app's refusals and rows from before stay
  as they were), is **in doubt for every later run** (the write ledger's `ledgerDoubts`) — not for its own run (its corrected try goes
  on: `exceptRun`) and not once a later write of its own run to the same target was `done`; it may be answered about
  (`confirm`, like `unknown`, past its window), and the view says `checked` and its `confirmableAt`. A late refusal after
  `unknown` ends the doubt only with `checked`. The app's card lists an unconfirmed refusal under 「分からない」 (not under
  「していないこと」) unless a later done write went to the same address, and offers ［済んでいた］［されていなかった］ for it.
- **The model**: `LedgerLife.tla` — a refused answer may come for a write that took effect; `AppChecksUnchanged` only when
  it did not; R20 `R20_NotSavedMeansNotLanded`; `BrokenLedgerRefusedTrusted` (a refusal no doubt) → R17,
  `BrokenLedgerUnchangedGuess` → R20.

## The stopped card's answer (Codex review point 6, owner-approved 2026-11-28)

［そのまま進める］ used to send the operator's question — a page could shape it — inside the member's own message to the
consultation AI, which has far wider tools: a way around the blindfold through the person's click.

- **Kept in main, bound to the card**: a stopped card that asks to decide (a `bo_` preview card or a `bf_` work card with
  `next.kind === 'decide'`) is remembered with its site, the Skill as the AI named it, its consultation and the question
  (`work.ts` `decidables`, memory only, cleared at sign-out). ［そのまま進める］／［やめる］ call `browserLines:decide(companyId,
  cardId, 'proceed' | 'stop')` (a window showing the consultation, as every card) and only then send the member's message:
  **fixed words and the card's id, never the question** (`stopAnswerMessage(answer, cardId)`).
- **The continuation**: the consultation AI calls `browser_work` again with `decision: <card id>` (`bo_` / `bf_`, nothing
  else); main continues only a card the member answered ［そのまま進める］, for the same site, Skill and consultation, within
  30 minutes, **once** — else `BROWSER_WORK_WORDS.badDecision`, nothing done. That run's operator gets `person.decided:
  [{ question (outside text), answer: 'はい（そのまま進める）' }]` and its promise `decisions: ['proceed']`. The consultation AI
  never sees the question (`askedFooter` tells it to pass the card's id). The answer is used up only once the new run
  really starts (a request refused before that — no window, busy, the Skill — keeps it).

Tests: `server/tests/unit/browser-run-promise.test.ts` (the verdict as a table — kept, broken by value / missing, asked for
fields / targets / values; an addition fills, never changes; the label rule; the readers' refusals; `body` / `bind` in
`browser.yaml`), `server/tests/unit/browser-frames.test.ts` "the run's promise" (made once before the first permit, the
trigger, strict against the run's frames, digests only; a bound field with another value or none never permitted; asked
items and the person's addition; a target this run made or promised; another run's not; only an open run with a promise,
≤ 20; no promise = as before; an AI plan carries no promise fields; the decision kept) and "sent is not done" (a refusal in
doubt for later runs, its own run going on, answerable; found unchanged → none; superseded by a done of its own run),
`browser-skill-draft.test.ts` (the drafted `body`), `client/tests/browser-frame-writes.test.ts` (the promise before any
permit, digests only, the preview's values only with a card; a bound value differing never sent nor asked; the card's
question — ［書かない］ nothing sent, ［書いてよい］ the addition then the permit, never given up as unknown; targets made / asked /
not asked twice; a refusal read again; 「済みました」 only when matched, `differs` and `not_read` apart, a missing promised
field unconfirmed; the stopped card's answer once, for its site and Skill, the question to the operator only),
`browser-stop-ask.test.ts` (the message's fixed words), `browser-window-electron.test.ts` (the completion regression with
the promise: nothing asked, matched), `model/browser-plan/check` (FramesLife R19 `R19_WithinPromise`, `BrokenFramesNoPromiseCheck`;
LedgerLife R20), the permission matrix and `CROSS` rows, `jobs-schema.test.ts`, the IPC inventory. Each guard
mutation-checked.

## The operator's tool sets (owner 2026-11-28: compare forms on the real app, chosen per PC)

The operator (no tool: one JSON action a step) is told its actions and reads back in one **tool set**. The owner wants to
compare forms on the real app (「どうせ私しか使わない」): `current` (today's — `click`, `type`, `select`, `go`, `back`, `wait`),
`compact` (`click`; **`set`** puts a value — typing into a field or a choice of a select, the element decides; **`go`**
takes a path of the site or `back`; `wait`), and three that **imitate a known tool set's words and shapes only** (checked
against their public documentation; sources in the PR that added them):
- `playwright` (Playwright MCP): `browser_click` (`target` = the ref, `element` a description, dropped),
  `browser_mouse_click_xy` (`x`, `y`: its vision tool), `browser_type` (`target`, `element`, `text`; **without `target`:
  the field pressed just before**, so a field with no ref can be typed into as today), `browser_select_option`
  (`values`: exactly one), `browser_navigate` (`url`: an `https` address of the run's domain on its default port, written in
  its plain form — nothing the URL reader would rewrite, such as `..`, a space or `\` — or a path; never another host, no
  user, no `#`), `browser_navigate_back`, `browser_wait_for` (`time`).
- `openai` (OpenAI computer use's action objects): `{"type":"click","button":"left","x","y"}` (only `left`),
  `{"type":"type","text"}`, `{"type":"wait"}` (no duration of its own: `OPENAI_WAIT_SECONDS` = 2).
- `anthropic` (Anthropic computer use, the `computer_20251124` input shape; the 2026-08 toolset's member tools carry the
  same names): `{"action":"left_click","coordinate":[x,y]}`, `{"action":"type","text"}`, `{"action":"wait","duration"}`.
- The two computer-use sets add **the app's own `do` words** for what a screen alone cannot reach in this app (it has no
  scroll or keys): `click` by `ref` (an element off the screen is scrolled into view, as today), `select`, `go`, `back`.
  Not offered in any set (the app cannot do them; an answer naming one is an unknown word, its options extra keys):
  double / right / middle / triple clicks, keys, scrolling, dragging, moving or holding the mouse, hovering, screenshots
  and zoom (the screen is sent every step), Playwright's `submit` / `slowly` / `modifiers` / `text` waits, files, tabs,
  scripts.
- **Typing without an element** (computer use's `type`, Playwright's `browser_type` without `target`): only when the last
  action other than a typing or a wait was a press that reached a text field (`pressedField`: `pressed.role` `textbox` —
  a password field has its own role); else the answer is refused (`focus`, asked again once in a write run), never
  guessed. Today's and the compact set keep their own rule (no ref = the focused field). The window still checks the
  focused element before typing, in every set.

- **One place** (`client/shared/browser-operator-toolsets.ts`, pure, no site word; the list, the setting's reading and the
  member's words in the leaf `browser-operator-toolset-choice.ts`, so the card and the settings share them without a
  cycle): each set's action lines and first screen line (only those differ: `browseOperatorPrompt`,
  `workPreviewOperatorPrompt`, `workOperatorPrompt` build every prompt; the current set's prompts are the constants as
  before), `readToolsetAnswer(set, text, { page, history, host })` (a set's answer → today's words, before the one
  parser), `toolsetHistory` (the history the operator is shown as its set would say it: the set's word under its key —
  `do`, `type`, `action` —, a ref as `target` in the Playwright set, a compact back as `go` + `path: "back"`).
  `browserOperatorArgs(model, prompt, toolset)` picks the set's fixed prompt (a closed choice; the preview of declared
  reads has one prompt for every set).
- **What a set changes, and only that** (owner: できることは同じ、守りも同じ): what the operator is told and how its answer is
  read. Every answer becomes one of today's `BrowserBrowseAction`s and goes through `parseBrowseAnswer` /
  `parseWorkAnswer` unchanged, so a password field, a wrong role, an option not offered, a long text, another site, a
  point off the image are refused exactly as today. Renaming is strict: a key the set does not have is refused (`keys`),
  never dropped or carried under another name (a key the reading would write that is already there included); a word
  the set does not have is an unknown word (`shape`, re-asked once in a write run as any slip). Everything after the
  parser — the window's checks before acting, the `ai` gate, each page write held and sent once within the frames, the
  permits, the run's promise, the wait for a press's write, the read-back — is the same code for every set. **The run log
  keeps today's words** (`browseLogStep` on the action as read; the server's `BROWSER_RUN_ACTIONS` unchanged).
- **Which set ran is this PC's only** (never the server's run log): the card's trail carries it (`BrowserTrailRun.toolset`,
  shown in the memo as 「係の道具：…」) and each operator run ends with a local log line
  (`[browser] operator run <id> (<phase>): tool set <set>, <outcome> (<reason>)`).
- **Said in the set's words**: a refused answer's re-ask and the card's 「アプリが止めました」 name the keys the set taught
  (`answerProblemWords(problem, toolset)`, `client/shared/browser-operator-trail.ts`; e.g. compact `set の value`,
  Playwright `target` / `values` / `browser_navigate の url`, OpenAI `button が "left"`, Anthropic `coordinate` /
  `duration`); a problem a set does not reword keeps today's words.
- **The setting** (this PC only, `config.json` `browserOperatorToolset`, default `current`; `config:get|setBrowserOperatorToolset`,
  a value not of the list refused and never stored, an unknown stored value read as `current`): Settings → ブラウザ操作 →
  「係の道具」 (`BrowserToolsetSection`, shown with a site): 「いまの形」／「まとめた形」／「Playwright 風」／「OpenAI 風」／
  「Anthropic 風」, saying that what the AI may do and the app's guards are the same and only how it is told changes. Read
  **once per request** (`toolset` dep of `wireBrowserBrowse` / `wireBrowserWork`, from main): a write task's preview and
  work use the same set.
- **Measured** (characters of the read / write preview / write prompts): current 853 / 1356 / 1331, compact 808 / 1311 /
  1286, playwright 1059 / 1562 / 1537, openai 835 / 1338 / 1313, anthropic 861 / 1364 / 1339. The completion regression
  runs in every set on the real window and prints each set's operator steps, work steps and stops (`t.diagnostic`); a
  scripted operator measures that the sets behave alike (a computer-use set takes one more step per typing: it presses
  the field first), not a model's choices — those are the owner's trial on the real app.

Tests: `client/tests/browser-operator-toolsets.test.ts` (every set's answer read as exactly today's action with its memo
keys; the one parser refuses a password field, a wrong role, an option not offered, a long text, another site — another
host, http, a port, a user, a fragment —, a point off the image, a key it would overwrite, a `__proto__` key; each set
refuses the other sets' words and the official actions and options the app cannot do; typing without an element only
after a press on a text field; the history in each set's words and keys; each set's prompts teach only its words and
keep every other line of today's; the operator started with the set's prompt only, an unknown value today's; the re-ask
in the set's words), `browser-browse.test.ts` (all five: the same actions in the window and the same log; the same
answers refused with nothing done; typing without an element after a button, a password field or nothing refused, after
a text field — also past a wait — typed), `browser-frame-writes.test.ts` (all five: the same actions and the one write
within the frame, a write outside the frames refused, each slip asked again once in the set's words), `browser-window-electron.test.ts`
(the completion regression in all five: the two writes once each, the promise, all ✓, no stop; the computer-use sets type
into the field they pressed on the real window), `OperatorTrail.test.ts` (the memo names the set and the broken rule in its
words), `browser-core-words.test.ts` (every set's prompts name no site), `ipc-app.test.ts` (default, every known set
stored, an unknown value refused), `ui/browser-settings.spec.ts` (「係の道具」: five choices, the default, a choice saved, a
failed save).

## Not yet (later PRs)

**The operator's window tools, later** (step ④'s follow-ups): an admin page for the run log (the API is there:
`GET /browser-runs`, its write steps included). **Writes, later** (step ⑤b's follow-ups): the Skill's steps from the
company's Skill shelf (a company without the shared folder: today the operator gets the task only), a file chooser the
person fills for an upload (design memo 追記: a file body is refused today), safe patterns (step ⑧).

**Keeping a login on disk**: built ("Keeping a login on disk" above). The four gaps PR 4's reviews found are closed
there: the record-only re-run, another company's or another agent's unenclosed turns (every turn asks first), and a
refused policy refresh (removes that company's).

The AI does not operate the window yet: the runner, the first type and the server's plans (6a,
above) exist but nothing starts a run. Still to come: **6b** — the operator AI and the preview's reads
are built (6b-1, "The operator and the preview", switched off) and the facts read for a plan (6b-2b,
"The facts a checker reads": the unconfirmed ones read as unknown until the owner's next login) and
the execution (6b-2c, "Executing a plan": claim → re-read → permit → send → report) and the registration,
the card, its IPC and the request tool (6b-2d, "The card, the IPC and the request tool"), all switched
off, and the inbox's rows (switched off too); **6c** — on for the owner's PC only through "This PC's
opt-in" (built, `lstep@0.6.3` approved: "Login helpers" lets the person log in, "Asset hosts" draws the page), and **the server's PC
registration** (the server's half, the app's half and the admin pages are built: "This PC's registration", "The admin pages: PCs and people"; still to come:
the end of the transition, D5) before it is on for anyone else;
`irreversible` types and more than one write (refused by the first version's rule today); **the method override and the swap** (design memo §24: a write may
declare `method: DELETE` / `PATCH` with `override: form_field`, sent as POST with `_method=<method>` in the
body; `matchDeclaredWrite` would then match the effective method and the declared `_method` pair, still
refusing `_method` from anything else and every override header; an effective DELETE needs `effect:
delete` and `irreversible: true`; then the swap is a declaration only — add then remove, `expect` add +
remove, target `{ reminder, step, hole }`, the checker confirming the hole is the step's and holds
`old`; a run cut between its two writes is partly done, never resent or rolled back), **checkers run
isolated and signed** and handed out with their declarations from the shelf (design memo §25: a
JavaScript engine without network, files or clock, limits on time and memory; only checkers div signed;
the package's `signature.yaml` verified first), landing routes (the read-only inspection's map 1.0.1 and line exist, the line `approved`, and the server takes its
plans and results: "The first map and the inspection line", "Plans of opened lines on the server"; the inspection is read in the
app and shown on its card since PR 5b, "The inspection in the chat"; creating routes is a later line), JSON writes, types with more than one write (their state table and model are in
"Executions with several writes"; the server's half is PR 4, unreachable until a line can write; the app's is PR 5), the shelf handing out browser connectors. WSS to the operated host stays refused (no type declares
one). Until the AI operates, the admin page says next to the mode choice what 「使う」 does today
(`BROWSER_NOT_YET_NOTE`); the PR that lets the AI operate removes that note (and its assertion in
`admin/tests/agent-browser.test.tsx`).
